{"batchcomplete":true,"query":{"pages":[{"pageid":52893575,"ns":0,"title":"The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts television series and event in 2017}}\n{{Infobox television\n|image = \n|genre = [[reality television|Reality]], [[Broadcasting of sports events|Sports]]\n|runtime = 60 minutes\n|creator = [[Frank Fertitta III]], [[Lorenzo Fertitta]], [[Dana White]]\n|starring = [[Dana White]], [[Cody Garbrandt]] and [[T.J. Dillashaw]]\n|country = United States\n|network = [[Fox Sports 1]]\n|first_aired = {{Start date|2017|04|19}}\n|last_aired = {{End date|2017|07|05}}\n}}\n\t\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 25''''') is an installment of the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC)-produced [[reality television]] series ''[[The Ultimate Fighter]]''.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/1/16/14282962/cody-garbrandt-tj-dillashaw-to-coach-the-ultimate-fighter-25-ufc-mma-news|title=Cody Garbrandt and T.J. Dillashaw to coach The Ultimate Fighter 25: Redemption|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2017-01-15|access-date=2017-01-16}}\n\nThe series was officially announced by the UFC on January 15, 2017 during the [[UFC Fight Night: Rodríguez vs. Penn]] broadcast. The then-current [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Cody Garbrandt]] would be coaching opposite former champion [[T.J. Dillashaw]]; Both of them being former training partners. As the twenty-fifth season of the American entries of the series, the producers have opted to bring back previous participants from the previous seasons.\n\nPer the UFC's announcement, the cast consists of welterweights that have all competed on ''The Ultimate Fighter'' on previous seasons; this includes former participants and winners of the show, as well as one current UFC fighter. The cast was announced on February 15, including former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger and [[The Ultimate Fighter 2]] welterweight winner [[Joe Stevenson]], [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Edgar vs. Team Penn]] middleweight winner [[Eddie Gordon (fighter)|Eddie Gordon]], as well as then-current UFC fighter [[James Krause (fighter)|James Krause]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/15/14630574/joe-stevenson-james-krause-jesse-taylor-among-tuf-redemption-cast-members|title=Joe Stevenson, James Krause, Jesse Taylor among TUF: Redemption cast members|work=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-02-15|access-date=2017-02-15}}\n\nThis season debuted on April 19, 2017 on Fox Sports 1.\n\n==Cast==\n===Coaches===\n{{Col-begin}}\n{{Col-break}}\n{{color box|#CC0000|border=silver}} '''Team Garbrandt''':\n*[[Cody Garbrandt]], Head Coach\n*[[Urijah Faber]]\n*[[Danny Castillo]]\n*[[Justin Buchholz]]\n*Fábio Prado\n*Robert Meese\n*Eddie Barraco\n\n{{Col-break}} {{color box|#0070FF|border=silver}} '''Team Dillashaw''':\n*[[T.J. Dillashaw]], Head Coach\n*[[Duane Ludwig]]\n*[[Eliot Marshall]]\n*Leister Bowling\n*[[Matt Brown (fighter)|Matt Brown]]\n*Robert Follis\n{{col-end}}\n\n===Fighters===\n*'''Team Garbrandt'''\n**[[Seth Baczynski]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Liddell vs. Team Ortiz|TUF 11]]), [[Mehdi Baghdad]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber|TUF 22]]), [[Eddie Gordon (fighter)|Eddie Gordon]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Edgar vs. Team Penn|TUF 19]]), [[Hayder Hassan]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians|TUF 21]]), Julian Lane ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Carwin vs. Team Nelson|TUF 16]]), [[Justin Edwards (fighter)|Justin Edwards]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos|TUF 13]]) and Johnny Nunez* ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber|TUF 22]])\n*'''Team Dillashaw'''\n**[[James Krause (fighter)|James Krause]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Live|TUF 15]]), [[Jesse Taylor]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rampage vs. Team Forrest|TUF 7]]), [[Ramsey Nijem]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos|TUF 13]]), [[Dhiego Lima]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Edgar vs. Team Penn|TUF 19]]), [[Joe Stevenson]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter 2|TUF 2]]), Tom Gallicchio ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber|TUF 22]]) and Gilbert Smith ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen|TUF 17]]).\n\n''*Nunez replaced [[Hector Urbina]] ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Edgar vs. Team Penn|TUF 19]]), after Urbina failed to make weight.''\n\n==Episodes==\n'''Episode 1: Redemption (April 19, 2017)'''{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/04/episode-no-1-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-25-redemption|title=Episode No. 1 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 25: Redemption'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-04-20|access-date=2017-04-20}}\n* The episode opens with a look at the personal struggles of this season's cast members, including \"TUF 2\" winner Joe Stevenson, Gilbert Smith and Tom Gallicchio. A number of other fighters also get a brief spotlight.\n* With all the competitors assembled together at the \"TUF\" gym, UFC President [[Dana White]] enters alongside this season's coaches. [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Cody Garbrandt]] will take the helm for one team while former champ [[T.J. Dillashaw]] will be the opposite coach.\n* White explains that the winner of this season will not only get another opportunity to make a career under the UFC banner, but he will also be rewarded with a $250,000 prize. Moreover, each athlete will be paid $10,000 per fight with a potential $5,000 bonus on the line for a stoppage victory. Beyond the standard single-elimination tournament format, White reveals a \"wild card\" fight will take place to close out the opening round. Two of the losing fighters will be selected for a matchup and the winner will be re-entered into the tournament.\n* The fighters immediately jump into a tryout training session. The athletes work out so the coaching staffs from each team can evaluate the talent before team picks are made. Garbrandt takes the approach of interviewing each fighter for a mental evaluation rather than just a physical test. He wants to know their goals, weight and any other details that will determine their success or failure on the show.\n* With evaluations complete, the team selections are underway. A coin toss will determine which side gets to pick the first fight or the first fighter. White also announces that teams will alternate fight picks, regardless of who wins the previous fight. Team Dillashaw wins the coin toss and opts to pick the first fighter.\n* '''Fighters selection:'''\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n!Coach\n!1st Pick\n!2nd Pick\n!3rd Pick\n!4th Pick\n!5th Pick\n!6th Pick\n!7th Pick\n|-\n!Dillashaw\n|James Krause\n|Jesse Taylor\n|Ramsey Nijem\n|Dhiego Lima\n|Joe Stevenson\n|Tom Gallicchio\n|Gilbert Smith\n|-\n!Garbrandt\n|Seth Baczynski\n|Mehdi Baghdad\n|Eddie Gordon\n|Hector Urbina\n|Hayder Hassan\n|Julian Lane\n|Justin Edwards\n|}\n* Garbrandt announces the first fight and matches his first pick Baczynski against Dillashaw's last pick Smith. Since the tournament will start with back-to-back fights, Dillashaw picks his first choice Krause to face Garbrandt's fourth pick Urbina.\n* At the first team Garbrandt training session, there's already some concern about Urbina's weight. He came on the show nearly 200 pounds and has limited time to make it all the way down to the 171-pound welterweight limit.\n* After two days of weight cut, Urbina is on the cusp of giving up. He does not think it is realistic for him to make weight and he tells coach Garbrandt that he's officially pulling out of the fight, as he still weighted 188.4 pounds. Garbrandt says he does not regret picking Urbina but feels a lifestyle change is needed if he wants to continue a career in high level MMA. White arrives at the gym and asks Urbina to tell the entire cast and all the coaches that he's out of the fight. Urbina apologizes to everyone involved, but White shows no leniency and tells Urbina to pack his bags and leave the competition immediately. \n* White pulls the coaches to an office, where they begin a conversation about what happens in the fallout of Urbina's departure. White says there are alternate fighters standing by outside the house in the event of the current situation, and Johnny Nunez (TUF 22) is one of the athletes available to join the cast. Dillashaw believes he should be able to pick an entirely new fight because of how the coin toss played out, but White shuts down that idea and Garbrandt criticizes Dillashaw for \"trying to play the victim, like he’s going to do all season.\"\n* Dillashaw informs Krause of the situation and the fighter is noticeably unhappy. He does not believe the fight date should be pushed back and is frustrated that he had to cut weight for no reason. He tries to avoid dwelling on the situation but says the change is not ideal for his approach to the tournament as a whole.\n* Shortly thereafter, Nunez arrives at the gym to join his new teammates for the first time. He's grateful for the opportunity and plans to make up for the disappointment of his first run.\n* Back at the house, Smith's intensity begins to gear up ahead of his fight with Baczynski. He feels he's viewed as an easy target after being picked last by his team and pitted in the first fight and believes the other team is wrong by picking him first. Smith's talk continues at the gym and Baczynski overhears everything from the neighboring locker room. He confronts Smith and some heated words are exchanged between the two sides. It briefly fizzles, but when Smith attempts to apologize, tensions flare up once again and the two athletes come face-to-face and exchange more words.\n* Garbrandt calls team Dillashaw \"a team full of fakes\" and that's when Dillashaw becomes irritated. He turns to Garbrandt and fires back with some words of his own, telling his rival to \"get the (expletive) out of my face.\" In a stunning move, Garbrandt then grabs Dillashaw by the throat and shoves him backward. The surrounding fighters immediately jump in and separate the coaches before any serious follow-up physicality can occur. Dillashaw appears unflustered by the situation and says the move was the perfect indictment on Garbrandt’s personality. Garbrandt feels differently, as he believes his opponent is shaken and scared.\n* '''Gilbert Smith''' defeated Seth Baczynski via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* The next fight is announced by Garbrandt: Eddie Gordon vs. Tom Gallicchio.\n\n'''Episode 2: Eat Some Souls (April 26, 2017)'''\n* While training at the ''TUF'' gym, things get fiery between Dillashaw and Garbrandt on the way to the team locker rooms when the alleged story of Dillashaw's leaving [[Team Alpha Male]], Urijah Faber's training camp, gets out. Both coaches have their sides of the story, and things escalate quickly, causing their fighters to separate them. Even Faber, who was, as he said, \"cool\" with Dillashaw, joins in the fray, calling his former fighter's version a bunch of \"bold-faced lies\".\n* '''Tom Gallicchio''' defeated Eddie Gordon via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n* The next fight is announced by Dillashaw: Jesse Taylor vs. Mehdi Baghdad.\n\n'''Episode 3: Iron Sharpens Iron (May 3, 2017)'''\n* '''Jesse Taylor''' defeated Mehdi Baghdad via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* The next fight is announced by Dillashaw: James Krause vs. Johnny Nunez.\n\n'''Episode 4: Piece of Us (May 10, 2017)'''\n\n*The fighters on Team Dillashaw create a unique [[birthday card]] made out of their body hair for coach T.J. and brings it to the gym, along with a cake.\n*At the ''TUF'' house, Cody invites Urijah Faber, UFC Featherweight fighter [[Clay Guida]], former UFC Bantamweight champion [[Miesha Tate]], and his girlfriend \"Lunchbox\" to watch the latest UFC event. During the fights, Julian Lane, a former bartender, started mixing drinks and gets Cody, who does not drink alcohol much, drunk. \n*Cody hangs with da bois\n* '''James Krause''' defeated Johnny Nunez via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n* The next fight is announced by Garbrandt: Hayder Hassan vs. Dhiego Lima.\n\n'''Episode 5: A Brutal Business (May 17, 2017)'''\n*In order to help his team, Garbrandt brings in Dr. Jon Petrick to perform a [[craniofacial]] release, a ballooning of the nose to open up the nasal passages, helping more oxygen get to the brain. Urijah Faber goes first and it is not a pleasant experience.\n* '''Dhiego Lima''' defeated Hayder Hassan via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* The next fight is announced by Dillashaw: Ramsey Nijem vs. Julian Lane.\n\n'''Episode 6: Let Me Bang! (May 24, 2017)'''\n\n*Things get instense between Ramsey Nijem and Juilian Lane during their stare down at the weight-in. So much so that the assistant coaches from each team, Justin Buchholz and Duane Ludwig get into an argument over their respective gyms and how Duane believes he's the one who turned T.J. into a champion. They, along with Cody and T.J. have to be separated.\n* '''Ramsey Nijem''' defeated Julian Lane via TKO (punches) in the first round.\n* After the fight, Cody complains that the referee stopped the fight way too early, especially when Lane was dominating the fight early in the round, making for an intense match. Team Dillashaw is now a perfect 6-0.\n* The next fight is announced by Garbrandt: Joe Stevenson vs. Justin Edwards.\n\n'''Episode 7: Dark Horse (May 31, 2017)'''\n* '''Justin Edwards''' defeated Joe Stevenson via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* Dana White asks Cody and T.J. their thoughts on what two fighters they think deserve a second chance in the wild card fight. Based on who will be the best and go the furthest in the UFC, Dana picks Joe Stevenson and Hayder Hassan. And Cody isn't happy with Dana's decision.\n* Feeling like T.J. picked his fighter Stevenson after being a \"legend\" in the UFC, Cody does not think Stevenson is ready to fight in only six days rest due to taking \"too many shots\" in his last fight. Cody's fighter Eddie Gordon also argues with T.J. that he should have deserved a wild card pick as well. \n* The wild card fight is announced by Dillashaw: Joe Stevenson vs. Hayder Hassan.\n\n'''Episode 8: Kryptonite (June 7, 2017)'''\n* '''Hayder Hassan''' defeated Joe Stevenson via KO (punch) in the first round.\n* The quarter final match-ups are announced as:\n:*Tom Gallicchio vs. Justin Edwards\n:*Gilbert Smith vs. Dhiego Lima\n:*Jesse Taylor vs. Hayder Hassan\n:*James Krause vs. Ramsey Nijem\n*With a break in training, it’s time for the annual \"Coaches Challenge.\" This season, head coaches Dillashaw and Garbrandt clash in a game of water [[tetherball]]. The winning coach receives $10,000 while each member of their team gets a $1,500 prize.\n*Both fighters admit they have minimal experience playing tetherball and Dillashaw falls into the water before the game even begins. Garbrandt takes a dominant 8–4 lead in the game up to 10, but Dillashaw stages a huge rally to tie it at 8 apiece. Dillashaw completes his comeback of six consecutive points and pulls off the victory. He wins the prize for himself and his team, and he says of Garbrandt, \"I watched him give up.\"\n* '''Tom Gallicchio''' defeated Justin Edwards via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n\n'''Episode 9: Killashaw (June 14, 2017)'''\n* Both coaches reward their teams and their fighters getting into the quarter finals by renting a party bus to take them to have dinner at a [[hibachi]] Japanese steakhouse. But Gilbert Smith is a party-pooper and does not partake in the celebration, believing he hasn't anything to celebrate yet and wants to concentrate on his upcoming fight.\n*After Team Grabrandt defiles T.J.'s pictures hanging in the gym by using red tape to make a forked snake tongue over his mouth, T.J. pranks back with custom t-shirts for his team. The logo is a snake with the name \"Killashaw Team Ninja\" across it, along with the motto, \"strike hard, strike fast, no mercy\".\n* '''Dhiego Lima''' defeated Gilbert Smith via unanimous decision after three rounds.\n* Following the loss, Smith says he's going to retire from MMA competition after a more than six-year career. He shares a moment with the coaches and fighters from both sides before leaving his gloves in the center of the octagon.\n\n'''Episode 10: Time to Smash (June 21, 2017)'''\n* During the first fight weight-in, once again Dillashaw and Garbrandt's quarreling over why Dillashaw left Team Alpha Male escalate and their assistant coaches get in on it, taking away the focus on their fighters. \n* '''Jesse Taylor''' defeated Hayder Hassan via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n* '''James Krause''' defeated Ramsey Nijem via unanimous decision after three rounds.\n* The semi final match-ups are announced as:\n:* Dhiego Lima vs. Tom Gallicchio\n:* James Krause vs. Jesse Taylor\n\n'''Episode 11: Embrace the Snake (June 28, 2017)'''\n\n* With James Krause injured in his last fight due to a cornea scratch, Dana tells Cody he has the choice of picking Krause's replacement in the event the physician does not clear him to fight. \n* Ramsey Nijem is upset because he believes he should move onto the finals in Krause's place. His complaining angers Gilbert Smith so much so that he confronts Ramsey and starts to argue but backs down once he realises Ramsey isn't intimidated. \n* T.J. decides to get back at Cody for pranking him and paints Team Grabrandt's locker room in Team Dillashaw's colors and hangs pictures of himself up. But Cody's fighters quickly make it a \"snake free zone\" once again before Cody gets back.\n*T.J.'s prank goes a step further when he embraces Cody's nickname for him; the snake, by putting live snakes in Team Grabrandt's locker room.\n* '''Dhiego Lima''' defeated Tom Gallicchio by unanimous decision after three rounds.\n\n'''Episode 12: The Winner's Circle (July 5, 2017)'''\n\n* T.J. brings in ''TUF 24'' winner [[Tim Elliott]] to help with James Krause who is his coach, teammate and friend. But Elliot overstays his welcome at the ''TUF'' house when his inappropriate comments about a \"winner's circle\" rub the losing fighters the wrong way, especially Julian Lane and Justin Edwards who get in his face and push him out the door.\n* Krause goes to the eye physician to get his [[corneal abrasion]] checked. It turns out 80 percent of his eye skin was taken off during his last bout, however, it healed nicely and the doctor clears him to fight.\n* After Elliot and the rest of Krause's training partners leave, Seth Baczynski gets into a pushing altercation with Krause as to why all of Krause's teammates were in the house in the first place.\n* '''Jesse Taylor''' defeated James Krause via technical submission (guillotine choke) in the third round.\n* In the finale: ''TUF 19'' runner-up Dhiego Lima vs. ''TUF 7'' Jesse Taylor who is 7–0 in the cage of the ''TUF'' gym.\n\n==Tournament Out\n|}\n{{Clear}}\n\n==The Ultimate Fighter 25 Finale==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption Finale\n|image=TUF_25_Finale.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting gate|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-08|access-date=2017-07-08}}\n|gate=$432,495\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Chiesa vs. Lee]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 213|UFC 213: Romero vs. Whittaker]]\n}}\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption Finale''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 25 Finale''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on July 7, 2017 at [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas metropolitan area]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/3/14499400/a-closer-look-at-the-ufcs-2017-schedule|title=A closer look at the UFC’s 2017 schedule|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-02-03|access-date=2017-02-03}}\n\n===Background===\nThe event was headlined by a lightweight bout between [[Michael Johnson (fighter)|Michael Johnson]] and former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]] [[Justin Gaethje]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/?p=247085|title=Justin Gaethje's UFC debut set vs. Michael Johnson in TUF 25 Finale headliner in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-12}}\n\nThe welterweight finals of ''The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption'' took place at the event.\n\nPromotional newcomer Amanda Ribas was expected to face [[Juliana Lima]] at the event, but Ribas was flagged by [[USADA]] for a potential anti-doping violation and removed from the bout. The possible violation stems from a sample collected June 7.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/6/15/15814246/amanda-ribas-flagged-by-usada-for-potential-anti-doping-violation-out-of-tuf-25-finale|title=Amanda Ribas flagged by USADA for potential anti-doping violation, out of TUF 25 Finale|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-06-15|access-date=2017-06-15}} She was replaced by [[Tecia Torres]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/6/20/15843798/tecia-torres-replaces-amanda-ribas-fights-juliana-lima-at-tuf-25-finale|title=Tecia Torres replaces Amanda Ribas, fights Juliana Lima at TUF 25 Finale|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2017-06-20|access-date=2017-06-21}}\n\n[[Steve Bossé]] was expected to face [[Jared Cannonier]] at the event. However, Bossé was removed from the fight just days before the event and was replaced by promotional newcomer Nick Roehrick.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamad.com/2017/07/04/steve-bosse-out-nick-roehrick-makes-ufc-debut-against-jared-cannonier-at-the-tuf-25-finale/|title=Steve Bossé out, Nick Roehrick makes UFC debut against Jared Cannonier at the TUF 25 Finale|publisher=mmamad.com|author=Dale Jordan|date=2017-07-04|access-date=2017-07-04|url-status=usurped|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170911191024/http://www.mmamad.com/2017/07/04/steve-bosse-out-nick-roehrick-makes-ufc-debut-against-jared-cannonier-at-the-tuf-25-finale/|archive-date=2017-09-11}}\n\n[[Aspen Ladd]] was expected to face [[Jessica Eye]] at the event, but on the day of the fight, she was pulled due to being ill and the fight was canceled.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/jessica-eye-aspen-ladd-scratched-canceled-tuf-25-finale|title=Jessica Eye vs. Aspen Ladd scratched from tonight's TUF 25 Finale lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-07|access-date=2017-07-07}}\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent Lewis vs. Hunt]] \n}}\n'''''UFC 212: Aldo vs. Holloway''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on June 3, 2017, at the [[Jeunesse Arena]] in [[Rio de Janeiro]], [[Brazil]].{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-212-jose-aldo-and-max-holloway-set-to-unify-title-021017|title=UFC 212: José Aldo and Max Holloway set to unify title|work=foxsports.com|author=Iolando Neto|date=February 10, 2017|access-date=February 10, 2017}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] unification bout between two-time champion [[José Aldo]] and interim champion [[Max Holloway]] served as the main event.{{cite news|url=http://www.flocombat.com/article/51845-jose-aldo-vs-max-holloway-set-for-ufc-212-in-brazil#.WJ6R247QfIV|title=José Aldo vs. Max Holloway set for UFC 212 in Brazil|publisher=flocombat.com|author=Jim Edwards|date=February 10, 2017|access-date=February 11, 2017}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]] was expected to face former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] at the event. However, on April 6, 2017, he was pulled from the bout after he was flagged for a potential doping violation by [[USADA]] stemming from his [[UFC Fight Night: Belfort vs. Gastelum|most recent bout]] against [[Vitor Belfort]]. Gastelum tested positive for Carboxy-Tetrahydrocannabinol (Carboxy-THC) which is a metabolite of [[marijuana]] or [[hashish]] above the 180 ng/mL allowance by the [[World Anti-Doping Agency]] (WADA) standard. The in-competition sample was collected on the night of Gastelum's fight on March 11 in Brazil. He has been placed on a provisional suspension as a result of the positive drug test and he will be afforded a full adjudication process.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/kelvin-gastelum-pulled-from-fight-with-anderson-silva-after-testing-positive-for-marijuana-040617|title=Kelvin Gastelum pulled from fight with Anderson Silva after testing positive for marijuana|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=April 6, 2017|access-date=April 6, 2017}}\n\nAs of April 10, 2017, it was confirmed that former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and UFC Middleweight Champion [[Luke Rockhold]] was offered the fight and accepted it.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/?p=241987|title=Luke Rockhold offered to fight Anderson Silva at UFC 212; Dana White says it's 'a process'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn and Ken Hathaway|date=April 10, 2017|access-date=April 10, 2017}} In turn, Silva dismissed the idea a day later, citing that Rockhold was coming from a loss and because of that it wouldn't make sense. Instead, Silva floated potential matchups with [[Uriah Hall]] or a rematch with semi-retired [[Nick Diaz]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/04/anderson-silva-dismisses-potential-rockhold-belfort-matchups-at-ufc-212-open-to-romero-or-diaz-rematch|title=Anderson Silva dismisses potential Rockhold, Belfort matchups at UFC 212, open to Romero or Diaz rematch|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=April 11, 2017|access-date=April 11, 2017}} On May 1, Silva gave the UFC an ultimatum: he wants to fight [[Wrestling at the 2000 Summer Olympics|2000 Olympic silver medalist]] and former world champion in [[freestyle wrestling]] [[Yoel Romero]] for the interim title or he will retire.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/5/1/15508766/fired-up-anderson-silva-says-hell-retire-if-ufc-doesnt-book-interim-title-fight-against-yoel-romero|title=Fired-up Anderson Silva says he'll retire if UFC doesn't book interim title fight against Yoel Romero|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=May 1, 2017|access-date=May 3, 2017}} In turn, despite having two months to secure an opponent, Silva and promotion officials confirmed on May 11 that he would not compete at the event.{{cite news|url=http://www.olimpomma.com/anderson-silva-nao-lutara-no-ufc-212/|title=Anderson Silva announces he will not fight at UFC 212|publisher=olimpomma.com|author=Gabriel Carvalho|access-date=May 8, 2017|date=May 8, 2017|language=pt}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/no-anderson-silva-at-ufc-212|title=Yup, it's official: No Anderson Silva at UFC 212|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=May 11, 2017|access-date=May 11, 2017}}\n\n[[Felipe Arantes]] was expected to face [[Iuri Alcântara]] at the event. However, on May 11, Arantes was pulled from the fight due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Brian Kelleher]].{{cite news|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2017/05/sertanejo-sai-e-iuri-marajo-vai-encarar-estreante-brian-kelleher-no-ufc-rio-8.html|title=Sertanejo out, Iuri Marajó will face newcomer Brian Kelleher at UFC Rio 8|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone and Marcelo Russio|access-date=May 11, 2017|date=May 11, 2017|language=pt}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2]] welterweight winner [[Leonardo Santos (fighter)|Leonardo Santos]] and [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] was scheduled for this event, but was scrapped on May 18 after Aubin-Mercier got injured and Santos decided to pull out of the event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/olivier-aubin-mercier-vs-leonardo-santos-pulled-from-ufc-212-in-rio-de-janeiro|title=Olivier Aubin-Mercier vs. Leonardo Santos pulled from UFC 212 in Rio de Janeiro|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=May 18, 2017|date=May 18, 2017}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent 212|UFC 212: Aldo vs. Holloway]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Holm vs. Correia]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Hunt''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 110''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on 11 June 2017 at the [[Spark Arena]] in [[Auckland]], [[New Zealand]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/ufc-returning-to-new-zealand-for-ufc-fight-night-110-in-auckland-in-june|title=UFC returns to New Zealand in June for UFC Fight Night 110 in Auckland|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-03-05|access-date=2017-03-05}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the promotion has hosted in Auckland, following [[UFC Fight Night: Te Huna vs. Marquardt]] in June 2014.\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Derrick Lewis]] and the [[K-1 World Grand Prix 2001 Final|2001 K-1 World Grand Prix]] winner and former interim title contender [[Mark Hunt]] served as the main event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/derrick-lewis-vs-mark-hunt-targeted-for-new-zealands-ufc-fight-night-110-headliner|title=Derrick Lewis vs. Mark Hunt targeted for New Zealand's UFC Fight Night 110 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp and John Morgan|date=2017-03-20|access-date=2017-03-20}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Joseph Benavidez]] was expected to face [[Ben Nguyen]] at the event. However, Benavidez pulled out of the fight citing a knee injury on 10 May.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/joseph-benavidez-injury-ufc-fight-night-100-withdrawal|title=Joseph Benavidez says he's injured, out of UFC Fight Night 110 bout vs. Ben Nguyen|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-10|access-date=2017-05-10}} He was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter: Tournament of Champions]] flyweight winner and fellow former title challenger [[Tim Elliott]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/tim-elliott-replaces-joseph-benavidez-against-ben-nguyen-at-ufc-fight-night-110|title=Tim Elliott replaces Joseph Benavidez against Ben Nguyen at UFC Fight Night 110|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-05-10|access-date=2017-05-11}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3]] middleweight winner [[Warlley Alves]] was expected to face [[Kiichi Kunimoto]], but pulled out on May 18 due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by [[Zak Ottow]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/?p=248198|title=Zak Ottow replaces Warlley Alves to take on Kiichi Kunimoto at UFC Fight Night 110|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-18|access-date=2017-05-18}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Nadia Kassem]] was scheduled to face [[JJ Aldrich]], but pulled out on May 31 and was replaced by fellow newcomer Chan-Mi Jeon.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/19-year-old-chan-mi-jeon-nadia-kassem-ufc-fight-night-110|title=19-year-old Chan-Mi Jeon replaces Nadia Kassem next week at UFC Fight Night 110|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-31|access-date=2017-05-31}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Jeon came in at 118 lb, two pounds over the women's strawweight limit of 116 lb. As a result, she was fined 20% of the purse, which went to Aldrich and the bout proceeded as scheduled at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-fight-night-110-early-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream|title=UFC Fight Night 110 early weigh-in results: One fighter misses, but Lewis, Hunt at 265|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-06-09|access-date=2017-06-09}}\n\nJust hours before the event, [[Thibault Gouti]] pulled out of a scheduled lightweight bout against [[Dong Hyun Ma]] due to illness, prompting Kim's removal as well.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/thibault-gouti-ruled-out-of-ufc-fight-night-110-with-illness-hours-before-event|title=Thibault Gouti ruled out of UFC Fight Night 110 with illness hours before event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-06-10|access-date=2017-06-10}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Night: Chiesa vs. Lee\n|image= UFC_Oklahoma.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2017|06|25}}\n|venue= [[Chesapeake Energy Arena]]\n|city= [[Oklahoma City, Oklahoma]]\n|attendance=7,605{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-fight-night-112-draws-reported-7605-attendance-for-549302-live-gate|title=UFC Fight Night 112 draws reported 7,605 attendance for $549,302 live gate|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-06-26|access-date=2017-06-26}}\n|gate=$549,302\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Holm vs. Correia]]\n|followingevent=[[The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption#The Ultimate Fighter 25 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption Finale]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Chiesa vs. Lee''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 112''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on June 25, 2017, at [[Chesapeake Energy Arena]] in [[Oklahoma City, Oklahoma]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-announces-June-event-in-Oklahoma-City|title=UFC announces June event in Oklahoma City|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2017-03-22|access-date=2017-03-22}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the promotion has hosted in Oklahoma City, following [[UFC Fight Night: Diaz vs. Guillard]] in September 2009.\n\nA lightweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Live]] lightweight winner [[Michael Chiesa]] and [[Kevin Lee]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/3/30/15132432/michael-chiesa-returns-to-face-kevin-lee-in-ufc-fight-night-112-main-event|title=Michael Chiesa returns to face Kevin Lee in UFC Fight Night 112 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-03-30|access-date=2017-03-30}}\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight]] and [[UFC Welterweight Championship|Welterweight Champion]] [[B.J. Penn]] and [[Dennis Siver]] took place at this event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/b-j-penn-dennis-siver-johny-hendricks-tim-boetsch-at-ufc-fight-night-112-in-oklahoma-city|title=B.J. Penn-Dennis Siver, Johny Hendricks-Tim Boetsch at UFC Fight Night 112 in Oklahoma City|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-03-22|access-date=2017-03-22}} The pairing was originally booked for [[UFC 199]]. However, the bout was cancelled due to Siver being injured.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/5/11/11654852/ufc-199-fight-card-dennis-siver-injured-vs-b-j-penn|title=Dennis Siver injured, out of UFC 199 fight opposite B.J. Penn|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2016-05-11|access-date=2017-03-30}}\n\n[[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]] was expected to face [[Ilir Latifi]] at the event. However, on May 17, it was announced that Nogueira pulled out due to a neck injury. Subsequently, despite having six weeks to secure a replacement opponent, promotion officials elected to remove Latifi from the event entirely.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/5/17/15655942/minotouro-nogueira-injured-ufc-fight-night-112-ilir-latifi|title='Minotouro' Nogueira injured, out of UFC Fight Night 112 fight with Ilir Latifi|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2017-05-17|access-date=2017-05-17}}\n\nA featherweight bout between promotional newcomers, [[Jared Gordon]] and Michel Quiñones was expected to take place at [[UFC 211]]. However, Gordon pulled out of the fight on the day before the event due to stomach illness and as a result, Quiñones was removed from the card.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/jared-gordon-ufc-211-with-food-poison-symptoms|title=Jared Gordon off Saturday's UFC 211 card due to 'food poison symptoms'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-12}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/jared-gordon-michel-quinones-joachim-christensen-azamat-murzakanov-ufc-fight-night-112|title=Jared Gordon vs. Michel Quinones rebooked, Christensen-Murzakanov added to UFC Fight Night 112|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-25|access-date=2017-05-25}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Azamat Murzakanov]] was expected to face [[Joachim Christensen]] at the event. However, Murzakanov was removed from the card on June 8 for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Dominick Reyes]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamad.com/2017/06/09/azamat-murzakanov-out-of-bout-with-joachim-christensen-dominick-reyes-now-makes-ufc-debut/|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170630175602/http://www.mmamad.com/2017/06/09/azamat-murzakanov-out-of-bout-with-joachim-christensen-dominick-reyes-now-makes-ufc-debut/|url-status=usurped|archive-date=June 30, 2017|title=Azamat Murzakanov out of bout with Joachim Christensen, Dominick Reyes now makes UFC debut|publisher=mmamad.com|author=Dale Jordan|date=2017-06-09|access-date=2017-06-09}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Johny Hendricks]] and [[Jared Gordon]] missed the required weight for their respective fights, weighing in at 188 lb and 149 lb, respectively. They were both fined 20% of their purse which went to their respective opponents, [[Tim Boetsch]] and Michel Quiñones. The bouts proceeded at catchweight.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-fight-night-112-early-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-10-a-m-et|title=UFC Fight Night 112 weigh-in results: 2, including Johny Hendricks, come in heavy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-06-24|access-date=2017-06-24}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Kevin Lee]]\n|def.\n|[[Michael Chiesa]]\n|Technical Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|1\n|4:37\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (188 lb)\n|[[Tim Boetsch]]\n|def.\n|[[Johny Hendricks]]\n|TKO (head kick and punches)\n|2\n|0:46\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Felice Herrig]]\n|def.\n|[[Justine Kish]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–26, 30–26, 29–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Dominick Reyes]]\n|def.\n|[[Joachim Christensen]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|1\n|0:29\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Tim Means]]\n|def.\n|[[Alex Garcia (fighter)|Alex Garcia]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Dennis Siver]]\n|def.\n|[[B.J. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $1,225,000.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-fight-night-112-salaries-b-j-penn-earns-highest-purse-despite-tough-loss|title=UFC Fight Night 112 salaries: B.J. Michael Chiesa: $36,000\n* '''Tim Boetsch:''' $154,000 (includes $67,000 win bonus) def. Johny Hendricks: $80,000 ¹\n* '''Felice Herrig:''' $50,000 (includes $25,000 win bonus) def. Justine Kish: $14,000\n* '''Dominick Reyes:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Joachim Christensen: $16,000\n* '''Tim Means:''' $78,000 (includes $39,000 win bonus) def. Alex Garcia: $31,000\n* '''Dennis Siver:''' $78,000 (includes $39,000 win bonus) def. B.J. Penn: $150,000\n* '''Clay Guida:''' $110,000 (includes $55,000 win bonus) def. Michel Quiñones: $12,000 ²\n* '''Tony Martin:''' $38,000 (includes $19,000 win bonus) def. Johnny Case: $23,000\n* '''Jeremy Kimball:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Josh Stansbury: $12,000\n\n¹ ''Johny Hendricks was fined 20 percent of his purse ($20,000) for failing to make the required weight for his fight with Tim Boetsch. That money was issued to Boetsch, an OSAC official confirmed.''
\n² ''Jared Gordon was fined 20 percent of his purse ($2000) for failing to make the required weight for his fight with Michel Quiñones. That money was issued to Quiñones, an OSAC official confirmed.''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn June 29, it was announced that Chiesa filed a complaint against referee [[Mario Yamasaki]] and an appeal of the result, citing referee Yamasaki's \"long history of failing to meet the standards officials are held to\" in his request. He also mentioned illegal elbows delivered by Lee and Yamasaki's stoppage of the fight; Chiesa stated \"I did not tap, I did not verbally submit, I was not knocked unconscious, and at no point did I go unconscious.\" Oklahoma State Athletic Commission (OSAC) administrator Joe Miller confirmed he has received the request and will review the bout before potentially presenting it to the commission.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/michael-chiesa-appeals-ufc-fight-night-112-loss-mario-yamasaki-failure|title=Michael Chiesa appeals UFC Fight Night 112 loss, cites Yamasaki failure to meet referee standards|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-06-29|access-date=2017-06-29}} On July 20, the OSAC decided there \"wasn’t enough evidence\" to overturn Yamasaki's decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/osac-dismisses-michael-chiesas-appeal-ufc-fight-night-112|title=OSAC dismisses Michael Chiesa's appeal to overturn controversial UFC Fight Night 112 loss|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Fernanda Prates and John Morgan|date=2017-07-20|access-date=2017-07-20}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2017 in Night: Saffiedine vs. Lim]] which was at the [[Marina Bay Sands]] in January 2014.\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and former title challenger [[Bethe Correia]] headlined this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/4/11/15262796/holly-holm-bethe-correia-targeted-ufc-fight-night-111-main-event-singapore|title=Holly Holm vs. Bethe Correia targeted for UFC Fight Night 111 Caceres]] was linked to a bout with promotional newcomer Wang Guan at the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/singapore-ufc-fight-night-111-lineup-official-430-am-et-start-time|title=Singapore's UFC Fight Night 111 lineup is official – with a 4:30 a.m. ET start web|url=https://www.rappler.com/sports/171625-rolando-gabriel-dy-ufc-debut/|title=Filipino fighter Rolando Gabriel Dy gets his UFC shot|publisher=rappler.com|author=Ryan Songalia|date=2017-06-01|access-date=2017-06-01}}\n\n[[Jonathan Meunier]] was expected to face [[Li Jingliang]] at the event. However, Meunier's removal from the card was announced on June 6 due to an alleged injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Frank Camacho]].{{cite news|url=https://mmadna.nl/jonathan-meunier-out-frank-camacho-in-tegen-li-jingliang-tijdens-ufc-singapore/|title=Jonathan Meunier out, Frank Camacho in against Li Jingliang during UFC Singapore|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-06-06|access-date=2017-06-06|language=nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Carls John de Tomas came in at 131 lb, five pounds over the flyweight limit of 126 lb. As a result, he was fined 30% of his purse, which went to [[Naoki Inoue]] and their bout proceeded as scheduled at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-fight-night-111-early-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-9-p-m-et|title=UFC Fight Night 111 early weigh-in results: Headliner cleared, one prelim fighter Ultimate Fighter 25 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption Finale]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Nelson vs. Ponzinibbio]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 213: Romero vs. Whittaker''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on July 8, 2017, at [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas metropolitan area]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/ufc-requests-3-straight-july-dates-at-t-mobile-arena-in-las-vegas-for-international-fight-week|title=UFC requests 3 straight July dates at T-Mobile Arena in Las Vegas for International Fight Week|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-03-22|access-date=2017-03-22}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event took place during the UFC's annual International Fight Week.{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/2017-UFC-International-Fight-Week-headlined-by-UFC-213-and-two-day-fan-experience|title=UFC International Fight Week headlined by UFC 213 and exciting two-day ufc fan experience|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2017-04-08|access-date=2017-04-08}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Cody Garbrandt]] and former champion [[T.J. Dillashaw]] was expected to take place at this event, possibly as the headliner.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/04/dana-white-confirms-bantamweight-champ-cody-garbrandt-meets-former-champ-t-j-dillashaw-at-ufc-213|title=Dana White confirms bantamweight champ Cody Garbrandt meets former champ T.J. Dillashaw at UFC 213|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-04-09|access-date=2017-04-09}} However, on May 23, Garbrandt withdrew due to a back injury and the bout was scrapped.{{cite web|url=https://bleacherreport.com/articles/2710774-tj-dillashaw-vs-cody-garbrandt-scratched-from-ufc-213|title=TJ Dillashaw vs. Cody Garbrandt scratched from UFC 213|publisher=bleacherreport.com|author=Rob Goldberg|date=2017-05-23|access-date=2017-05-23}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] and multiple-time [[muay thai]] world champion [[Valentina Shevchenko (fighter)|Valentina Shevchenko]] was scheduled to headline this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/5/3/15532896/amanda-nunes-vs-valentina-shevchenko-in-the-works-for-ufc-213|title=Amanda Nunes vs. Valentina Shevchenko in the works for UFC 213|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2017-05-03|access-date=2017-05-04}} The pairing met previously in March 2016 at [[UFC 196]], with Nunes winning by unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/ufc-196-results-amanda-nunes-outlasts-valentina-shevchenko-for-decision-win|title=UFC 196 results: Amanda Nunes outlasts Valentina Shevchenko for decision win|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Ben Fowlkes|date=2016-03-05|access-date=2017-05-04}} However, the bout was canceled just hours before the event started due to Nunes being ill.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/7/8/15941740/amanda-nunes-ill-out-of-ufc-213-main-event|title=Amanda Nunes ill, out of UFC 213 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-07-08|access-date=2017-07-08}}\n\nAn interim [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between [[Wrestling at the 2000 Summer Olympics|2000 Olympic silver medalist]] and former world champion in [[freestyle wrestling]] [[Yoel Romero]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] was expected to serve as the co-headliner.{{cite web|url=https://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/yoel-romero-vs-robert-whittaker-interim-middleweight-title-fight-set-for-ufc-213-052317|title=Yoel Romero vs. Robert Whittaker interim middleweight title fight set for UFC 213|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2017-05-23|access-date=2017-05-23}} Due to the cancelation of the Nunes-Shevchenko bout, this fight was announced as the new main event.\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Robbie Lawler]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger [[Donald Cerrone]] was originally booked for [[UFC 205]]. However, Lawler pulled out to take a little more time after losing his title via knockout at [[UFC 201]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2016/9/10/12871178/robbie-lawler-vs-donald-cerrone-scratched-from-ufc-205|title=Robbie Lawler vs. Donald Cerrone scratched from UFC 205|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-09-10|access-date=2017-04-04}} The fight was rescheduled to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/4/3/15168952/ufc-213-robbie-lawler-vs-donald-cerrone-mma-news|title=Lawler set to face Cerrone at UFC 213 in July|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Tim Burke|date=2017-04-03|access-date=2017-04-03|archive-date=2017-04-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170404030255/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/4/3/15168952/ufc-213-robbie-lawler-vs-donald-cerrone-mma-news|url-status=dead}} Reports began to circulate on June 28 that Cerrone had sustained a minor injury and that the pairing would be left intact, but expected to shift to [[UFC 214]] three weeks later.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/6/28/15885554/donald-cerrone-injured-robbie-lawler-bout-likely-moving-to-ufc-214|title=Donald Cerrone injured, Robbie Lawler bout likely moving to UFC 214|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Luke Thomas|date=2017-06-28|access-date=2017-06-28}} UFC President [[Dana White]] confirmed later that Cerrone in fact had a [[staph infection]] and a [[pulled muscle|pulled groin]], and while the plan was to keep the bout, it wouldn't happen at UFC 214.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/dana-white-donald-cerrone-injured-off-ufc-213-ufc-214-robbie-lawler|title=Dana White: Donald Cerrone too injured to make up Robbie Lawler fight at UFC 214|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Simon Samano and John Morgan|date=2017-06-28|access-date=2017-06-28}} In the end, the bout was confirmed on July 2 for UFC 214.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/donald-cerrone-robbie-lawler-rebooked-ufc-214-dana-white|title=Report: Donald Cerrone vs. Robbie Lawler rebooked for UFC 214 after all|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-02|access-date=2017-07-02}}\n\n[[Alan Jouban]] was expected to face [[Brian Camozzi]] at the event. However, on June 6, it was announced that Jouban pulled out due to a broken foot.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/6/6/15750222/alan-jouban-out-of-brian-camozzi-bout-foot-injury-ufc-213-fight-card|title=Alan Jouban out of UFC 213 bout against Brian Camozzi due to foot injury|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2017-06-06|access-date=2017-06-06}} He was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter Nations: Canada vs. Australia]] welterweight winner [[Chad Laprise]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/chad-laprise-replaces-alan-jouban-meets-brian-camozzi-at-ufc-213|title=Chad Laprise replaces Alan Jouban, meets Brian Camozzi at UFC 213|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-06-08|access-date=2017-06-08}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Douglas Silva de Andrade]] and [[Rob Font]] was originally booked for [[UFC 175]] in July 2014. However, de Andrade was pulled from the bout due to injury and replaced by [[George Roop]]. The bout was then rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/rob-font-douglas-silva-de-andrade-official-ufc-213|title=Rob Font vs. Douglas Silva de Andrade official for UFC 213|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-06-21|access-date=2017-06-21}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Robert Whittaker '''Chad Laprise'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $2,596,000.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/ufc-213-salaries-fight-purses-money-alistair-overeem|title=UFC 213 final salaries: Alistair Overeem earns event-high $800,000 disclosed payday|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-09|access-date=2017-07-09}} \n* '''Robert Whittaker:''' $350,000 (no win bonus) def. Yoel Romero: $350,000\n* '''Alistair Overeem:''' $800,000 (no win bonus) def. Fabrício Werdum: $275,000\n* '''Curtis Blaydes:''' $38,000 (includes $19,000 win bonus) def. Daniel Omielańczuk: $30,000\n* '''Anthony Pettis:''' $180,000 (includes $90,000 win bonus) def. Jim Miller: $71,000\n* '''Rob Font:''' $39,000 (includes $19,500 win bonus) def. Douglas Silva de Andrade: $18,000\n* '''Oleksiy Oliynyk:''' $54,000 (includes $27,000 win bonus) def. Travis Browne: $120,000\n* '''Chad Laprise:''' $48,000 (includes $24,000 win bonus) def. Brian Camozzi: $10,000\n* '''Thiago Santos:''' $68,000 (includes $34,000 win bonus) def. Gerald Meerschaert: $14,000\n* '''Belal Muhammad:''' $40,000 (includes $20,000 win bonus) def. Jordan Mein: $25,000\n* '''Cody Stamann:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Terrion Ware: $10,000\n* '''Trevin Giles:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. 214","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2017}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC 214: Cormier vs. Jones 2\n|image=UFC_214_event_poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2017|07|29}}\n|venue=[[Honda Center]]\n|city=[[Anaheim, California]]\n|attendance=16,610{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/ufc-214-cormier-vs-jones-2-sets-ufc-honda-center-attendace-record|title='UFC 214: Cormier vs. Jones 2' sets UFC-Honda Center {{sic|nolink=y|attendace}} record|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-30|access-date=2017-07-30}}\n|gate=$2,448,870\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC on Fox: Weidman vs. Gastelum]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Pettis vs. Moreno]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 214: Cormier vs. Jones 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] and held on July 29, 2017, at the [[Honda Center]] in [[Anaheim, California]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/24/14731214/ufc-214-in-anaheim-changed-to-july-29|title=UFC 214 in Anaheim changed to July 29|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2017-02-24|access-date=2017-04-21}}\n\n==Background==\nA PPV event was initially planned for Anaheim on January 21, 2017, but was rescheduled for this date after a lack of suitable headlining fights could be arranged for the initial date in January.\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 320\n | image1 = Daniel Cormier taking a picture with a fan..jpg \n | width1 = 2300| height1 = 2900\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = Jon Jones - Supporting Brain Health Study.jpg\n | width2 = 2300 | height2 = 3000\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer = This was the third time that the UFC booked a rematch between Cormier ''(left)'' and Jones ''(right)'', after previous attempts ([[UFC 197]] and [[UFC 200]]) were scrapped.\n}}\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Daniel Cormier]] and former champion [[Jon Jones]] had been targeted for this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/4/20/15379134/daniel-cormier-vs-jon-jones-2-targeted-for-ufc-214|title=Daniel Cormier vs. Jon Jones 2 targeted for UFC 214|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-04-20|access-date=2017-04-21}} The pairing met previously at [[UFC 182]] in January 2015 with Jones defending his title via unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/01/ufc-182-results-jon-jones-tested-but-defends-title-against-daniel-cormier|title=UFC 182 results: Jon Jones tested, but defends title against Daniel Cormier|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2015-01-04|access-date=2017-04-21}} Subsequent to that victory, Jones was stripped of the title and suspended indefinitely from the UFC in connection with a [[Jon Jones#Hit-and-run conviction|hit-and-run incident]] in which he was involved.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/4/28/8512255/jon-jones-stripped-of-title-daniel-cormier-vs-anthony-johnson-for|title=Jon Jones stripped of title, Daniel Cormier vs. Anthony Johnson for belt headlines UFC 187|author=Marc Raimondi|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2015-04-29|access-date=2017-04-21}} Cormier replaced him and went on to defeat [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] at [[UFC 187]] to win the vacant title.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/05/ufc-187-results-daniel-cormier-wears-down-anthony-johnson-for-third-round-tap|title=UFC 187 results: Daniel Cormier wears down Anthony Johnson for third-round tap|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2015-05-24|access-date=2017-04-21}} Their rematch was originally expected to take place at [[UFC 197]], but Cormier pulled out three weeks before the event due to injury and was replaced by [[Ovince Saint Preux]], in what became an interim title bout.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/4/2/11353572/jon-jones-vs-ovince-saint-preux-set-for-ufc-197-with-daniel-cormier|title=Jon Jones vs. Ovince Saint Preux set for UFC 197 with Daniel Cormier out|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-04-02|access-date=2017-04-21}} Jones went on to defeat Saint Preux by unanimous decision and won the interim title.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2016/4/24/11496188/ufc-197-results-jon-jones-ovince-st-preux|title=UFC 197 results: rusty Jon Jones shuts down Ovince St. Preux, wins interim light heavyweight title|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Thomas Myers|date=2016-04-24|access-date=2017-04-21}} The fight was scheduled again and was expected to headline [[UFC 200]] in July 2016. However, Jones was pulled out of the fight by [[USADA]] due to a potential Anti-Doping Policy violation stemming from an out-of-competition sample collection on June 16. Jones' \"B\" sample also came positive for the same substances and Jones was suspended for one year, retroactively to July 6, 2016.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/usada-testing-of-jon-jones-b-sample-confirms-initial-findings|title=USADA: Testing of Jon Jones' 'B' sample confirms initial findings|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-08|access-date=2017-04-21}}{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/jon-jones-suspended-one-year-by-usada-for-doping-violation-110716|title=Jon Jones suspended one year by USADA for doping violation|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-11-15|access-date=2017-04-21}} The Cormier-Jones rematch was officially announced on May 12, though its status as main event or co-headliner was not initially confirmed.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/one-more-time-daniel-cormier-vs-jon-jones-title-rematch-headlines-ufc-214-on-july-29|title=One more time: Daniel Cormier vs. Jon Jones title rematch set for UFC 214 on July 29|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-12}} UFC president [[Dana White]] confirmed that the bout would headline this event, despite previous issues regarding Jones. He also announced that [[Jimi Manuwa]] would participate on this card and should anything happen to the main event, Manuwa would be the replacement.{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Dana-White-CormierJones-Gets-Headlining-Status-at-UFC-214-Manuwa-on-Card-as-Backup-121389|title=Dana White: Cormier-Jones gets headlining status at UFC 214; Manuwa on card as backup|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-13}} On May 31, [[Volkan Oezdemir]] was announced as his opponent.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/volkan-oezdemir-gets-wish-fight-jimi-manuwa-ufc-214|title=Volkan Oezdemir gets his wish: UFC 214 fight with Jimi Manuwa|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-31|access-date=2017-05-31}}\n\nFormer [[List of Strikeforce champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|Strikeforce Women's Featherweight Champion]] and [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC Featherweight Champion]] [[Cristiane Justino]] (commonly referred to as Cris Cyborg) expressed her desire to face the inaugural [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]] [[Germaine de Randamie]] at this event. However, de Randamie indicated that she would not face Cyborg due to her repeated transgressions with [[performance enhancing drugs|PEDs]] (despite Cyborg only failing a test in 2011).{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/germaine-de-randame-refuses-cyborg-justino-fight-ufc-title-stripped|title=Germaine de Randamie refuses to fight 'Cyborg' Justino, willing to have UFC belt stripped|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2017-05-26|access-date=2017-06-19}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/ufc-champ-germaine-de-randamie-breaks-silence-return-135-pound-division|title=UFC champ Germaine de Randamie breaks silence, says 'focus' is on return to 135 pounds|author=Mike Bohn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2017-05-29|access-date=2017-06-19}} The UFC decided to strip her of the title on June 19 due to her refusal to defend it and then booked Cyborg against promotional newcomer and current Invicta FC Featherweight Champion [[Megan Anderson (fighter)|Megan Anderson]] for the vacant title.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-germaine-de-randamie-stripped-of-belt-unwillingness-fight-cyborg|title=UFC: Yes, Germaine de Randamie stripped of belt due to 'unwillingness' to fight 'Cyborg'|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2017-06-19|access-date=2017-06-19}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-214-cyborg-justino-megan-anderson-germaine-de-randamie-stripped|title='Cyborg' vs. Megan Anderson for title at UFC 214 after Germaine de Randamie stripped|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2017-06-19|access-date=2017-06-19}} In turn, Anderson pulled out on June 27 for personal reasons and was replaced by current [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Tonya Evinger]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Evinger-replaces-Anderson-to-fight-Cyborg-at-UFC-214|title=Evinger replaces Anderson to fight Cyborg at UFC 214|author=Thomas Gerbasi|publisher=ufc.com|date=2017-06-27|access-date=2017-06-27}}\n\nOn June 28, a [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Tyron Woodley]] and former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger and multiple-time [[jiu-jitsu]] world champion [[Demian Maia]] was announced as the third title fight of this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/tyron-woodley-to-defend-title-vs-demian-maia-at-ufc-214|title=Tyron Woodley to defend title vs. Demian Maia at UFC 214|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-06-28|access-date=2017-06-28}}\n\n[[File:Honda-pano2.jpg|thumb|370px|right|Home of the [[Anaheim Ducks]], the [[Honda Center]] hosted its seventh UFC event.]]\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challengers [[Ricardo Lamas]] and [[Chan Sung Jung]] was originally in the works for [[UFC on Fox: Weidman vs. Gastelum]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2017/3/31/15139500/report-korean-zombie-vs-ricardo-lamas-to-headline-ufc-on-fox-25-in-long-island|title=Korean Zombie vs. Ricardo Lamas in the works for UFC on FOX 25 in Long Island|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2017-03-31|access-date=2017-05-13}} However, on May 12, the bout was confirmed for this event.{{cite 29|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-13}} The pairing was originally slated to take place in July 2013 at [[UFC 162]], but Jung was pulled from the bout in favor of a title fight against then-champion [[José Aldo]] at [[UFC 163]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ricardo-Lamas-Pulled-From-UFC-162-in-Wake-of-Korean-Zombie-Title-Shot-53401|title=Ricardo Lamas pulled from UFC 162 in wake of 'Korean Zombie' title shot|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Mike Whitman|date=2013-06-19|access-date=2017-05-13}} Subsequently, Jung pulled out of the fight in early June due to a severe knee injury.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmanews.com/report-chan-sung-jung-off-ufc-214-card-with-an-injury/|title=Chan Sung Jung off UFC 214 card with an injury|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Fernando Quilles Jr.|date=2017-06-02|access-date=2017-06-02}}{{cite knee injury that knocked him out of UFC 214|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2017-06-06|access-date=2017-06-07}} He was replaced by [[Jason Knight (fighter)|Jason Knight]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Zombie-out-Knight-in-to-face-Lamas-UFC-214|title=Zombie out, Knight in to face Lamas at UFC 214|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2017-06-06|access-date=2017-06-06}}\n\n[[Doo Ho Choi]] was expected to face [[Andre Fili]] at the event, but pulled out on June 14 due to injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/doo-ho-choi-injured-andre-fili-ufc-214|title=Dooho Choi injured, but Andre Fili still plans to fight at UFC Puelles was expected to face [[Sage Northcutt]], but pulled out on June 19 due to a knee injury.{{cite injured|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2017-07-14|access-date=2016-07-14}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Robbie Lawler]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger [[Donald Cerrone]] was originally booked for [[UFC 205]]. However, Lawler pulled out to take a little more time after recovering from his previous fight at [[UFC 201]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/9/10/12871178/robbie-lawler-vs-donald-cerrone-scratched-from-ufc-205|title=Robbie Lawler vs. Donald Cerrone scratched from UFC 205|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-09-10|access-date=2017-04-04}} The fight was then rescheduled to take place on July 8, 2017, at [[UFC 213]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/4/3/15168952/ufc-213-robbie-lawler-vs-donald-cerrone-mma-news|title=Lawler set to face Cerrone at UFC 213 in July|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Tim Burke|date=2017-04-03|access-date=2017-04-03|archive-date=2017-04-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170404030255/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/4/3/15168952/ufc-213-robbie-lawler-vs-donald-cerrone-mma-news|url-status=dead}} Reports began to circulate on June 28 that Cerrone had sustained a minor injury and that the pairing would be left intact, but shifted to this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/6/28/15885554/donald-cerrone-injured-robbie-lawler-bout-likely-moving-to-ufc-214|title=Donald Cerrone injured, Robbie Lawler bout likely moving to UFC 214|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Luke Thomas|date=2017-06-28|access-date=2017-06-28}} UFC President [[Dana White]] confirmed later that Cerrone in fact had a [[staph infection]] and a [[pulled muscle|pulled groin]], and while the plan was to keep the bout, it wouldn't happen at this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/dana-white-donald-cerrone-injured-off-ufc-213-ufc-214-robbie-lawler|title=Dana White: Donald Cerrone too injured to make up Robbie Lawler fight at UFC 214|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Simon Samano and John Morgan|date=2017-06-28|access-date=2017-06-28}} In the end, the bout was confirmed on July 2 for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/donald-cerrone-robbie-lawler-rebooked-ufc-214-dana-white|title=Report: Donald Cerrone vs. Robbie Lawler rebooked for UFC 214 after all|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-02|access-date=2017-07-02}}\n\nOn June 28, the [[California State Athletic Commission]] (CSAC) denied former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Renan Barão]] a bout at that weight class against [[Aljamain Sterling]], due to his previous weight issues at [[UFC 177]], in [[Sacramento, California]]. The bout still happened, but at a catchweight of 140 pounds. CSAC Executive Director Andy Foster said that if Barão makes weight with no issues, the CSAC will revisit the possibility of allowing him at 135 lb in the future.{{cite web|url=http://www.flocombat.com/article/58202-ufc-214-shakeup-renan-barao-vs-aljamain-sterling-no-longer-bantamweight#.WVPt2khtnIV|title=UFC 214 shakeup: Renan Barão vs. Aljamain Sterling no longer bantamweight|publisher=flocombat.com|author=Duane Finley|date=2017-06-28|access-date=2017-06-28}}\n\n[[Sara McMann]] and [[Ketlen Vieira]] were expected to face each other at the event, but on July 7 it was announced that the bout was moved to [[UFC 215]] in September.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/7/7/15939564/sara-mcmann-vs-ketlen-vieira-moved-to-ufc-215-edmonton|title=Sara McMann vs. Ketlen Vieira moved to UFC 215 in Edmonton|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-07|access-date=2017-07-07}}\n\nA heavyweight fight between [[Dmitrii Smolyakov]] and promotional newcomer Adam Wieczorek was scheduled for the preliminary portion of the event. However, the bout was removed from the card for undisclosed reasons on July 17 and is expected to be rescheduled for a future event in October.{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/ufc-gdansk-kent-met-adam-wieczorek-vs-dmitrii-smoliakov-eerste-gevecht/|title=UFC Gdansk knows first fight with Adam Wieczorek vs. Dmitrii Smoliakov|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-07-18|access-date=2017-07-19|language=nl}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Light Heavyweight|[[Daniel Cormier]] (c)|vs.|[[Jon Jones]]|NC (overturned)|3|3:01|For the [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]]. Josh Burkman: $54,000\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn August 22, it was announced that Jones was flagged for a potential doping violation by [[USADA]] stemming from his urine test sample collected after fight weigh-ins on July 28. He tested positive for [[Turinabol]], an [[anabolic steroid]]. Jones was placed on a provisional suspension as a result of the positive drug test pending the full adjudication process.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/8/22/16187202/jon-jones-failed-drug-test-at-ufc-214|title=Jon Jones failed drug test at UFC 214|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-08-22|access-date=2017-08-22}} The \"B\" sample from his pre-fight drug test later confirmed the presence of a banned substance, as announced on September 12. According to the [[California State Athletic Commission]] (CSAC), Jones passed multiple out-of-competition tests leading up to the event, as well as a blood test on July 29. That blood test does not significantly aid Jones' case, however, as blood tests do not test for Turinabol.{{cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/20687157/jon-jones-b-sample-confirms-failed-drug-test-ufc-214|title=Jon Jones' 'B' sample confirms failed drug test from UFC 214|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2017-09-13|access-date=2017-09-13}} On the following day, the CSAC officially changed the result to a no contest.{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/CSAC-Overturns-Jon-Jones-UFC-214-Triumph-vs-Daniel-Cormier-to-No-Contest-126903|title=CSAC overturns Jon Jones' UFC 214 triumph vs. Daniel Cormier to no contest|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2017-09-13|access-date=2017-09-13}}\n\nOn September 13, the UFC announced that Jones was stripped of the title and Cormier was being reinstated as champion.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/13/16305374/daniel-cormier-reinstated-as-ufc-light-heavyweight-champion|title=Daniel Cormier reinstated as UFC light heavyweight champion|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-09-13|access-date=2017-09-13}} On February 28, 2018, the CSAC revoked Jones' fight license and fined him a total of $205,000, including 40% of his disclosed salary. He can re-apply for a license after his case with USADA is concluded.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2018/02/california-state-athletic-commission-csac-fines-jon-jones-205000-revokes-license-failed-ufc-214-drug-test|title=CSAC fines Jon Jones $205,000, revokes license for failed UFC 214 test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2018-02-28|access-date=2018-02-28}}\n\nOn September 20, USADA announced that Jones received a 15-month suspension, after an evidentiary hearing with an independent arbitrator on September 15. The independent arbitrator, well-known anti-doping investigator Richard McLaren, found Jones had \"some degree of fault\" for the steroid metabolite that showed up in his system, but \"the violation was not intended nor could it have enhanced\" his performance. The suspension is retroactive to the date his positive sample was collected, making him eligible on October 28.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/ufc-jon-jones-receives-15-month-usada-suspension-after-arbitration|title=Jon Jones gets 15-month USADA suspension after arbitration, eligible for UFC return Oct. 28|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2018-09-19|access-date=2018-09-20}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2017 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist|30em}}\n\n{{UFC Ponzinibbio]]\n| followingevent = [[UFC 214|UFC 214: Cormier vs Jones 2]]\n}}\n'''''UFC on Fox: Weidman vs. Gastelum''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 25''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on July 22, 2017, at the [[Nassau Veterans Memorial Coliseum]] in [[Uniondale, New York]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/3/31/15138516/ufc-on-fox-25-set-for-nassau-coliseum-on-long-island|title=UFC on FOX 25 set for Nassau Coliseum on Long Island|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-03-31|access-date=2017-03-31}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the UFC has hosted on [[Long Island]]. \n\nThe event was headlined by a middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Chris Weidman]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/?p=247089|title=Long Island's own Chris Weidman set for UFC on FOX 25 vs. Kelvin Gastelum in July|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-12}}\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challengers [[Ricardo Lamas]] and [[The Korean Zombie|Chan Sung Jung]] was originally in the works to headline or co-headline this event.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2017/3/31/15139500/report-korean-zombie-vs-ricardo-lamas-to-headline-ufc-on-fox-25-in-long-island|title=Korean Zombie vs. Ricardo Lamas in the works for UFC on FOX 25 in Long Island|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2017-03-31|access-date=2017-04-20}} The pairing was originally slated to take place in July 2013 at [[UFC 162]], but Jung was pulled from the bout in favor of a title fight against then champion [[José Aldo]] at [[UFC 163]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ricardo-Lamas-Pulled-From-UFC-162-in-Wake-of-Korean-Zombie-Title-Shot-53401|title=Ricardo Lamas pulled from UFC 162 in wake of ‘Korean Zombie’ title shot|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Mike Whitman|date=2013-06-19|access-date=2017-04-20}} On May 12, the bout was confirmed for [[UFC 214]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2017/5/12/15631656/report-korean-zombie-vs-ricardo-lamas-official-for-ufc-214-on-july-29|title=Report: Korean Zombie vs. Ricardo Lamas official for UFC 214 on July 29|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-13}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Gian Villante]] and [[Patrick Cummins (fighter)|Patrick Cummins]] was originally booked for [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Abdurakhimov]] in December 2016. However, Cummins pulled out a week before the event due to a [[staph infection]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/patrick-cummins-announces-hes-out-of-ufc-fight-night-101-with-staph-and-the-video-is-gross|title=Patrick Cummins announces he's out of UFC Fight Night 102 with staph – and the video is gross|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-02|access-date=2017-04-20}} The fight eventually took place at this event.{{cite news|url=https://www.newsday.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/gian-villante-to-face-patrick-cummins-in-first-ufc-event-at-nassau-coliseum-i80408|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170420234756/http://www.newsday.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/gian-villante-to-face-patrick-cummins-in-first-ufc-event-at-nassau-coliseum-1.13494690|url-status=live|archive-date=April 20, 2017|title=Gian Villante to face Patrick Cummins in first UFC event at Nassau Coliseum|publisher=newsday.com|author=Mark LaMonica|date=2017-04-19|access-date=2017-04-20}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between Christian Colombo and [[Damian Grabowski]] was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Gustafsson vs. Teixeira]]. However, the pairing was scrapped as both fighters sustained injuries in the weeks leading up to the event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/injuries-christian-colombo-damian-grabowski-ufc-fight-night-109-sweden|title=Injuries scrap Christian Colombo vs. Damian Grabowski at UFC Fight Night 109 in Sweden|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-16|access-date=2017-05-24}} The fight was rescheduled to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamad.com/2017/05/19/damian-grabowski-vs-christian-colombo-rescheduled-for-ufc-on-fox-25-in-long-island/|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170606072601/http://www.mmamad.com/2017/05/19/damian-grabowski-vs-christian-colombo-rescheduled-for-ufc-on-fox-25-in-long-island/|url-status=usurped|archive-date=June 6, 2017|title=Damian Grabowski vs. Christian Colombo rescheduled for UFC on FOX 25 in Long Island|publisher=mmamad.com|author=Dale Jordan|date=2017-05-19|access-date=2017-05-24}} Subsequently, Colombo was removed from the fight on June 22 for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Chase Sherman]].{{cite
