event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on July 16, 2017, at [[The SSE Hydro]] in [[Glasgow]], [[Scotland]].{{cite news|url=http://www.dailyrecord.co.uk/sport/other-sports/mma/ufc-announce-glasgow-show-return-10038316|title=UFC announce Glasgow show as they return to Scotland for the second time|publisher=dailyrecord.co.uk|author=Tom Heffernan|date=2017-03-16|access-date=2017-04-21}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the promotion has hosted in Glasgow, following [[UFC Fight Night: Bisping vs. Leites]] in July 2015.\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Gunnar Nelson (fighter)|Gunnar Nelson]] and [[Santiago Ponzinibbio]] headlined the event.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/5/12/15629294/gunnar-nelson-vs-santiago-ponzinibbio-to-headline-ufc-fight-night-113|title=Gunnar Nelson vs. Santiago Ponzinibbio to headline UFC Fight Night 113|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-12}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/danny-henry-daniel-teymur-ufc-fight-night-113-scotland|title=UFC Fight Night 113 lineup set in Scotland, including new Danny Henry vs. Daniel Teymur booking|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-06|access-date=2017-07-06}}\n\n[[Mark Godbeer]] was expected to face promotional newcomer [[Justin Willis (martial artist)|Justin Willis]] at the event. However, Godbeer pulled out of the fight on May 21 citing injury and was replaced by fellow newcomer James Mulheron.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamad.com/2017/05/21/james-mulheron-signed-to-the-ufc-meets-justin-willis-at-ufc-fight-night-113-in-glasgow-following-mark-godbeer-injury/|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170606042947/http://www.mmamad.com/2017/05/21/james-mulheron-signed-to-the-ufc-meets-justin-willis-at-ufc-fight-night-113-in-glasgow-following-mark-godbeer-injury/|url-status=usurped|archive-date=6 June 2017|title=James Mulheron signed to the UFC, meets Justin Willis at UFC Fight Night 113 in Glasgow following Mark Godbeer injury|publisher=mmamad.com|author=Dale Jordan|date=2017-05-24|access-date=2017-05-24}}\n\n[[Lina Länsberg]] was expected to face [[Leslie Smith (fighter)|Leslie Smith]] at the event, but she pulled out on June 21 due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer Amanda Lemos.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/amanda-lemos-in-for-lina-lansberg-vs-leslie-smith-at-ufc-scotland|title=Report: Newcomer Amanda Lemos in for Lina Lansberg vs. Leslie Smith at UFC-Scotland|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-06-21|access-date=2017-06-21}}\n\n[[Mitch Gagnon]] was expected to face [[Brett Johns]] at the event. However, Gagnon was removed from the card on June 27 and replaced by [[Albert Morales]].{{cite news|url=https://champions.co/p/brett-johns-gets-new-opponent-for-ufc-glasgow/4306470|title=Brett Johns gets new opponent for UFC Glasgow|publisher=champions.co|author=Jim Edwards|date=2017-06-27|access-date=2017-06-29|archive-date=8 December 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171208174929/https://champions.co/p/brett-johns-gets-new-opponent-for-ufc-glasgow/4306470|url-status=dead}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Joanne Wood|Joanne Calderwood]] came in at 118 lb, two pounds over the strawweight limit of 116 lb. As a result, she was fined 20% of her purse, which went to [[Cynthia Calvillo]] and their bout proceeded as scheduled at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Fight-Night-113-Weighin-Video-Results-Joanne-Calderwood-Misses-Mark-124823|title=UFC Fight Night 113 weigh-in video & results: Joanne Calderwood misses mark|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Mike Sloan|date=2017-07-15|access-date=2017-07-15}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight vs. Ponzinibbio"}]},{"pageid":53953330,"ns":0,"title":"The Ultimate Fighter: A New World Champion","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts television series and event in 2017}}\n{{Infobox television\n|image = \n|genre = [[reality television|Reality]], [[Broadcasting of sports events|Sports]]\n|creator = [[Craig Piligian]], [[Frank Fertitta III]], [[Lorenzo Fertitta]], [[Dana White]]\n|starring = [[Dana White]], [[Eddie Alvarez]] and [[Justin Gaethje]]\n|country = United States\n|runtime = 60 minutes\n|network = [[Fox Sports 1]]\n|first_aired = {{Start date|2017|08|30}}\n|last_aired = {{End date|2017|11|29}}\n}}\n\t\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter 26''''' (also known as ''''' The Ultimate Fighter: A New World Champion''''') is an installment of the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC)-produced [[reality television]] series ''[[The Ultimate Fighter]]''.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/5/3/15538358/the-ultimate-fighter-26-to-crown-inaugural-ufc-womens-flyweight-champion|title=The Ultimate Fighter 26 to crown inaugural UFC women’s flyweight champion|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2017-05-03|access-date=2017-05-03}}\n\nTryouts were announced on May 3, scheduled for 20 days later. This season will exclusively feature the women's [[Flyweight (MMA)|flyweight]] division, with the goal of crowning the UFC's inaugural 125-pound champion in the season's finale. All women who were currently on the UFC roster were allowed to submit their names for the tournament, as were females unsigned by the UFC.\n\nThe news came just months after UFC president [[Dana White]] told reporters in Las Vegas that the UFC had no plans to institute any further women's divisions in 2017 other than women's featherweight. It also canceled plans the UFC previously had to hold a TUF 26 casting call for women's strawweight, women's bantamweight, and men's middleweight.\n\nThe coaches for this season were announced on July 13, pitting former two-time [[List of Bellator champions#Lightweight Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Eddie Alvarez]] against former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]] [[Justin Gaethje]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/justin-gaethje-eddie-alvarez-the-ultimate-fighter-26-coaches|title=Justin Gaethje, ex-UFC champ Eddie Alvarez to coach Season 26 of 'The Ultimate Fighter'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2017-07-13|access-date=2017-07-13}} The cast was officially announced on August 5, including inaugural [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Flyweight Championship|Invicta FC Flyweight Champion]] [[Barb Honchak]], [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate]] contestant [[Roxanne Modafferi]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] and current UFC fighter [[Lauren Murphy]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/ufc-ultimate-fighter-26-cast-revealed-inaugural-womens-flyweight-title|title='The Ultimate Fighter 26' cast revealed: Who's vying to become 1st UFC women's flyweight champ?|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-08-05|access-date=2017-08-06}}\n\n==Cast==\n===Coaches===\n{{Col-begin}}\n{{Col-break}}\n{{color box|#009E60|border=silver}} {{color box|#0BDA51|border=silver}} '''Team Alvarez''':\n* [[Eddie Alvarez]], Head Coach\n* Mark Henry\n* [[Zabit Magomedsharipov]]\n* [[Marlon Moraes]]\n* [[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]]\n* Rich Pohler\n\n{{Col-break}} {{color box|#A4DDED|border=silver}} {{color box|#273BE2|border=silver}} '''Team Gaethje''':\n* [[Justin Gaethje]], Head Coach\n* [[Trevor Wittman]]\n* [[Phil Nurse]]\n* [[Matthew Lopez (fighter)|Matthew Lopez]] \n* [[Luke Caudillo]]\n* Jacob Ramos\n* [[Vinny Magalhães]]\n* [[Miesha Tate]]\n {{col-end}}\n\n===Fighters===\n* '''Team Alvarez'''\n** [[Barb Honchak]], [[Lauren Murphy]], [[DeAnna Bennett]], [[Melinda Fábián]], Christina Marks, Ariel Beck, [[Sijara Eubanks]], and [[Shana Dobson]].\n* '''Team Gaethje'''\n** [[Roxanne Modafferi]], Maia Stevenson, [[Montana Stewart]], [[Emily Whitmire]], [[Rachael Ostovich|Rachael Ostovich-Berdon]], Karine Gevorgyan, [[Nicco Montaño]] and [[Gillian Robertson]].\n\n==Episodes==\n'''Episode 1: A New World Champion (August 30, 2017)'''\n\n* The season opens with a look back at preseason tryouts. A number of aspiring women's flyweight fighters gather in [[Las Vegas]] in the hopes of joining the cast in order to vie for the inaugural 125-pound belt in the UFC. UFC President [[Dana White]] discusses the tryouts, which attract more than 50 fighters from across the globe. White, along with matchmakers Sean Shelby and Mick Maynard, assess the athletes and decide on the 16 who will join the cast.\n* The fighters arrive at the \"TUF\" gym, where they are greeted by head coaches former two-time [[List of Bellator champions#Lightweight Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Eddie Alvarez]] and former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]] [[Justin Gaethje]], who are scheduled to fight at the end of the season. White summarizes the stakes of the tournament via a satellite message, and the competition is officially underway.\n* The fighters change into training gear for coaches evaluations. Gaethje interviews each fighter on top of grappling and pad work sessions. He says his priority is to select those with mental toughness and durability. Alvarez, meanwhile, has the fighters engage in a much more physical level of sparring. He wants to see the talent firsthand in a live-fight scenario and admits to being \"shocked\" by the level of talent on the cast.\n* At the conclusion of evaluations, Alvarez and Gaethje have the traditional coin toss to determine which team has the first fighter pick and which selects the first matchup. Alvarez wins the coin toss, and decides to pick the first fighter.\n* Since the fighters have been seeded by the UFC for the tournament (which was unknown to the coaches), as a coach makes a pick, the corresponding fighter based on seed will go to the other coach (e.g., if the #1 seeded fighter is picked by a coach, the #16 seeded fighter will go to the other coach).\n* The eight coaches' picks (and corresponding seed sent to the other team):\n\n:'''Barb Honchak''' – Ranked #2 (Team Alvarez – first pick)\n:'''Gillian Robertson''' – Ranked #15 (Team Gaethje)\n\n:'''Roxanne Modafferi''' – Ranked #1 (Team Gaethje – second pick)\n:'''Shana Dobson''' – Ranked #16 (Team Alvarez)\n\n:'''Lauren Murphy''' – Ranked #3 (Team Alvarez – third pick)\n:'''Nicco Montaño''' – Ranked #14 (Team Gaethje)\n\n:'''Rachael Ostovich-Berdon''' – Ranked #10 (Team Gaethje – fourth pick)\n:'''Melinda Fábián''' – Ranked #7 (Team Alvarez)\n\n:'''Sijara Eubanks''' – Ranked #12 (Team Alvarez – fifth pick)\n:'''Maia Stevenson''' – Ranked #5 (Team Gaethje)\n\n:'''Montana Stewart''' – Ranked #6 (Team Gaethje – sixth pick)\n:'''Ariel Beck''' – Ranked #11 (Team Alvarez)\n\n:'''DeAnna Bennett''' – Ranked #4 (Team Alvarez – seventh pick)\n:'''Karine Gevorgyan''' – Ranked #13 (Team Gaethje)\n\n:'''Emily Whitmire''' – Ranked #8 (Team Gaethje – eight pick)\n:'''Christina Marks''' – Ranked #9 (Team Alvarez)\n\n* Gaethje announces the first fight: #1 Roxanne Modafferi vs. #16 Shana Dobson.\n* The fighters then visit the \"TUF\" house for the first time. A few of the athletes are awestruck by house and platform they are about to compete on. The intensity and focus is high as the fighters settle into their new living area.\n* Modafferi, who is the first female to ever make two appearances on the \"TUF\" reality series, is eager to make up for a poor effort on [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate|Season 18]]. She was eliminated in the first round, but has since excelled under the [[Invicta FC]] banner and wants to prove to the world that she doesn’t \"suck anymore.\"\n* At the first Team Alvarez training session, the coaching staff beings to work with Dobson, who is a sizable underdog to Modafferi with just three career fights. She calls herself the \"best kept secret in MMA,\" and coach Alvarez says the key to victory is to keep the fight standing.\n* Team Gaethje has its first training session, and right off the bat coach Gaethje brings in a special assistant coach. Former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Women's Bantamweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Miesha Tate]] enters the gym. Tate was Modafferi's coach on Season 18, and gives her some motivational words.\n* '''Roxanne Modafferi''' defeated Shana Dobson via TKO (elbows) in the first round.\n* Alvarez announces the next match up: #4 DeAnna Bennett vs. #13 Karine Gevorgyan.\n\n'''Episode 2: Fight! (September 6, 2017)'''\n\n* After Roxanne Modafferi's win, Justin Gaethje and his assistant coaches visit the ''TUF'' house to celebrate her victory and they treat the whole team to a sushi dinner and playing games.\n* Justin has a hard time communicating with Karine Gevorgyan, who only knows a few words in English. He even uses her translator to describe techniques in preparation for her upcoming fight. But Karine feels left out being the only fighter from Armenia in the house. \n* During Team Gaethje's grappling session, Emily Whitmore injures herself, thinking that her rib popped out, and has to go to the hospital. UFC medical consultant Dr. Jeffrey Davidson diagnoses it as an [[intercostal muscle]] or cartilage strain to her rib area, nothing too serious that she can't fight in a few weeks.\n* Team Alvarez sees the new bracket board on the wall and the ladies now have in their heads if they win, they will fight a teammate, so Eddie Alvarez has to bring his team back together again.\n* '''DeAnna Bennett''' defeated Karine Gevorgyan via TKO (punches) in the first round.\n* Gaethje announces the next match up: #6 Montana Stewart vs. #11 Ariel Beck.\n\n'''Episode 3: Time to Shine (September 13, 2017)'''\n\n* Both teams visit the new UFC Performance Institute, a state-of-the-art training and wellness facility. They take advantage of their advanced equipment and technology. While there, Emily Whitmire gets her injured ribs checked out by the director of physical therapy, Heather Linden who uses compression tape and laser therapy to stabilize the pain.\n* Stressed about her upcoming fight, Ariel Beck suffers a [[panic attack]] during her training session, but she bounces back.\n* Team Gaethje gets dress up for a night out on the town when Gaethje takes them out to go bowling. To get into a competitive mindset, Gaethje sets up a contest between his team with a cash prize: Montana Stewart and Emily Whitmire team up and win the first prize of $200 and third prize with $100, and Rachael Ostovich-Berdon gets second prize with $150.\n* '''Montana Stewart''' defeated Ariel Beck via submission (D'Arce choke) in the first round.\n* Alvarez announces the next match up: #3 Lauren Murphy vs. #14 Nicco Montaño.\n\n'''Episode 4: The Truth in Me (September 20, 2017)'''\n* '''Nicco Montaño''' defeated Lauren Murphy via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* Gaethje announces the next match up: #5 Maia Stevenson vs. #12 Sijara Eubanks.\n\n'''Episode 5: Feelings Mean Nothing (September 27, 2017)'''\n\n* After her defeat, Lauren Murphy doesn't take losing lightly. She doesn't feel mentally and physically ready for practice and decides to skip morning training. Eddie Alvarez doesn't like her reaction, thinking she's being selfish and she's going to bring down the spirit and the morale of his team. Lauren disagrees and just needed a couple of hours to deal with her loss her way.\n* Maia Stevenson gets in her own head about why she was picked for the show; because her husband his ''TUF 2'' winner and former UFC fighter [[Joe Stevenson|Joe \"Daddy\" Stevenson]]. But her team assures her she got here on her own merits because of her MMA skills.\n* In order to make weight for her upcoming fight, Sijara Eubanks cuts her [[dreadlocks]] so she can lose the half a pound she needed to make weight at 125.\n* '''Sijara Eubanks''' defeated Maia Stevenson via submission (kimura) in the second round.\n* Alvarez announces the next match up: #2 Barb Honchak vs. #15 Gillian Robertson.\n\n'''Episode 6: Predator (October 4, 2017)'''\n* '''Barb Honchak''' defeated Gillian Robertson via TKO (punches and elbows) in the second round.\n* Gaethje announces the next match up: #7 Melinda Fábián vs. #10 Rachael Ostovich-Berdon.\n\n'''Episode 7: Enjoy the Moment (October 18, 2017)'''\n\n* Dana Whites surprise visits the women at ''TUF'' house to give them some motivation about signing with the UFC. A few fighters check out the features in his [[BMW 760i|BMW M760i]], one of 12 in the U.S.\n* '''Rachael Ostovich-Berdon''' defeated Melinda Fábián via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n* Gaethje announces the next match up: #8 Emily Whitmire vs. #9 Christina Marks.\n\n'''Episode 8: Sink or Swim (November 1, 2017)'''\n\n*After a tough loss she can't forget, Lauren Murphy is unhappy on Team Alvarez and resents Eddie on how he handled training leading up to her fight. She decides to train with Team Gaethje and the fighters and coaches welcome her. However, Eddie is personally insulted since he feels he has the credentials and the best coaches for his fighters. After Lauren calmly tells him about her joining the \"Blue Team\", Eddie goes on a tirade about it to his team and even gets Lauren's name wrong, calling her \"Barb\" and said \"she lost\". She even made him a thank you card and he refused to take it; his feelings were hurt.\n*Meisha Tate is back in town and helps her teammate Emily Whitmire with mending her injured ribs with a device called the Mac Pro. Meisha will also corner Emily's fight.\n*Eddie and Justin compete in the coaches challenge. This year's contest is swimming; Olympic Freestyle 50 meters (the length of 8 football fields). They must swim back and forth 16 time and must touch the swimming pool wall on both sides. The first coach to do it wins $10,000 and their team wins $1,500 each. Justin sets out on a fast pace while Eddie just takes his time. A quarter a way through, Justin gasses out and Eddie pulls ahead, winning the competition. Since Lauren left the \"Green Team\", the fighters took home $1,700. \n*'''Emily Whitmire''' defeated Christina Marks via verbal submission (armbar) in the first round.\n*Alvarez and Gaethje announce the quarterfinal match ups: \n: #1 Roxanne Modafferi vs. #8 Emily Whitmire. \n: #12 Sijara Eubanks vs. #4 Deanna Bennett. \n: #2 Barb Honchak vs. #10 Rachael Ostovich-Berdin. \n: #6 Montana Stewart vs. #14 Nicco Montaño.\n\n'''Episode 9: The Fight Gods (November 8, 2017)'''\n\n*So impressed with the preliminary fights, Dana White wants the women to relax and take their minds off fighting and decides to send them on a night cruise of [[Lake Las Vegas]] on ''La Contessa'' for some dinner and drinks. \n*'''Roxanne Modafferi''' defeated Emily Whitmire via TKO (elbows) in the first round.\n*During the second fight weigh-in, Sijara is a quarter pound overweight and has an hour to make weight. Meanwhile, Lauren Murphy seizes the opportunity to fight in her place by taking Sijara's sauna time, much to Alvarez's annoyance. However, Sijara makes the weight cut. \n*'''Sijara Eubanks''' defeated Deanna Bennett via KO (head kick) in the first round.\n\n'''Episode 10: Make it a Fight (November 15, 2017)'''\n\n*'''Barb Honchak''' defeated Rachael Ostovich-Berdin via unanimous decision in the second round.\n*'''Nicco Montaño''' defeated Montana Stewart via unanimous decision in the second round.\n\n'''Episode 11: A Will to Win (November 22, 2017)'''\n\n*While both teams train at the UFC Performance Institute, Eddie decides to play a prank on Justin since he left his car keys unattended. Eddie moves Justin's SUV and parks it in the lot closest to the highway up on the curb.\n*Justin gets his revenge on Eddie when he counter pranks him by lifting Eddie's SUV 15 feet off the ground via crane, writing \"Team Gaethje\" and \"I Love Justin\" on the windows, and also shooting the bottom of it with [[silly string]]. \n*'''Nicco Montaño''' defeated Barb Honchak via Unanimous Decision in the third round.\n\n'''Episode 12: The Ultimate Goal (November 29, 2017)'''\n\n*Constantly struggling to make weight before her fights, Sijara Eubanks gets help from the nutritionists at the UFC Performance Institute.\n*'''Sijara Eubanks''' defeated Roxanne Modafferi via Unanimous Decision in the third round. \n*Sijara moves on to the TUF 26 Finale to face Nicco Montaño.\n\n==Tournament Finale==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=The Ultimate Fighter: A New World Champion Finale\n|image=TUF_26_Finale.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2017|12|01}}\n|venue=[[Monte Carlo Resort and Casino#Rebranding|Park Theater]]\n|city= [[Paradise, Nevada]]\n|attendance=2,798{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2018/01/06/ufc-in-2017-ridiculously-robust-look-stats-streaks-skids-record-setters/82247360007/|title=UFC in 2017: A ridiculously robust look at the stats, streaks, skids and record-setters|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2018-01-06|access-date=2026-01-19}}\n|gate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Bisping vs. Gastelum]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 218|UFC 218: Holloway vs. Aldo 2]]\n}}\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter 26 Finale''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter: A New World Champion Finale''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on December 1, 2017, at [[Monte Carlo Resort and Casino#Rebranding|Park Theater]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas metropolitan area]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/3/14499400/a-closer-look-at-the-ufcs-2017-schedule|title=A closer look at the UFC’s 2017 schedule|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-02-03|access-date=2017-02-03}}\n\n[[Sijara Eubanks]] was expected to face [[Nicco Montaño]] in the finals of the season to determine the inaugural [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship|UFC Women's Flyweight Champion]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/the-ultimate-fighter-26-finale-tuf-26-title-fight-official-lineup|title=Tournament final/inaugural title fight set as part of Friday's official TUF 26 Finale lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-11-30|access-date=2017-11-30}} However, on the day of weigh ins, Eubanks was ruled medically ineligible for the card after a visit to the hospital overnight for allegedly miscalculating her [[weight cutting|weight cut]] and suffering kidney failure, which led promotion officials to replace her with semifinalist [[Roxanne Modafferi]], whom Eubanks defeated on the show.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/roxanne-modafferi-replaces-sijara-eubanks-nicco-montano-tuf-26-finale-inaugural-title-fight|title=Roxanne Modafferi replaces Sijara Eubanks in TUF 26 Finale's inaugural title fight|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp and John Morgan|date=2017-11-30|access-date=2017-11-30}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/tuf-26-finalist-sijara-eubanks-statement-weight-miss-ufc-title-shot|title='TUF 26' finalist Sijara Eubanks breaks silence on weight miss that scrapped UFC title shot|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2017-11-30|access-date=2017-11-30}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between promotional newcomer [[Priscila Cachoeira]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Lauren Murphy]] was expected to take place at the event.{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-Undefeated-Priscila-Cachoeira-Signs-With-UFC-Faces-Lauren-Murphy-in-December-127301|title=Undefeated Priscila Cachoeira signs with UFC, faces Lauren Murphy in December|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jordan Breen|date=2017-10-02|access-date=2017-10-02}} However, the pairing never materialized due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues for Cachoeira.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/11/30/16718964/lauren-murphy-not-fighting-at-tuf-26-finale-due-to-opponents-visa-issues|title=Lauren Murphy not fighting at TUF 26 Finale due to opponent’s VISA issues|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=MMA Fighting Newswire|date=2017-11-30|access-date=2017-11-30}} Murphy still weighed in as an alternate for any bout at the event and was then booked against former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Flyweight Championship|Invicta FC Flyweight Champion]] [[Barb Honchak]], who was left without an opponent after her scheduled opponent Modafferi was pulled in favor of the main event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/tuf-26-finale-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=TUF 26 Finale official weigh-in results: Sijara Eubanks ruled out of title fight|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-11-30|access-date=2017-11-30}}\n\nAt the weigh ins, Karine Gevorgyan weighed in at 130 pounds, 4 pounds over the flyweight non-title fight upper limit of 126 pounds. As such, the bout proceeded at a catchweight and Gevorgyan forfeited 30% of her purse to her opponent [[Rachael Ostovich]].\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on August 5, 2017, at [[Mexico City Arena|Arena Ciudad de México]] in [[Mexico City]], [[Mexico]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/ufc-returns-to-mexico-city-aug-5-ufc-fight-night-114|title=UFC announces return to Mexico City on Aug. 5, likely UFC Fight Night 114|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-11|access-date=2017-05-21}}\n\n==Background==\nA flyweight bout between [[Sergio Pettis]] and [[Brandon Moreno]] headlined the event.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufcespanol.com/news/ufc-mexico-2017-pettis-moreno-anuncio|title=Pettis vs Moreno headline in Mexico|publisher=ufcespanol.com|author=Juan Cárdenas|date=2017-05-21|access-date=2017-05-21|language=es}}\n\n[[Chris Gruetzemacher]] was expected to face [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America 3]] lightweight winner [[Martín Bravo (fighter)|Martín Bravo]] at the event. However, Gruetzemacher pulled out of the fight on July 18 with an injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Humberto Bandenay]].{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/chris-gruetzemacher-out-humberto-bandenay-in-tegen-martin-bravo-tijdens-ufc-mexico-city/|title=Chris Gruetzemacher out, Humberto Bandenay against Martín Bravo during UFC Mexico City|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-07-19|access-date=2017-07-19|language=nl}}\n\nA featherweight contest between [[Hacran Dias]] and [[Zabit Magomedsharipov]] was scheduled to take place at this event, but Magomedsharipov was moved to a fight against [[Nick Hein]] at the [[UFC Fight Night: Struve vs. Volkov]] event in Rotterdam in September.{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/zabit-magomedsharipov-debuteert-niet-in-mexico-maar-in-rotterdam-tegen-nick-hein/|title=Zabit Magomedsharipov won't debut in Mexico, will fight in Rotterdam instead against Nick Hein|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-07-20|access-date=2017-07-20|language=nl}} In turn, Hacran Dias was removed from the card entirely.\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Alexa Grasso]] came in at 119 lb, three pounds over the strawweight limit of 116 lb. As a result, she was fined 20% of her purse, which went to [[Randa Markos]] and their bout proceeded at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/alexa-grasso-misses-weight-ufc-fight-night-114-randa-markos|title=Video: Alexa Grasso misses weight for UFC Fight Night 114 co-headliner with Randa Markos|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-08-04|access-date=2017-08-05}}\n\nThis was the last event called by longtime color commentator and former fighter [[Brian Stann]].\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Flyweight\n|[[Sergio Pettis]]\n|def.\n|[[Brandon Moreno]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (49–46, 48–46, '''Humberto Bandenay''', '''Dustin Ortiz''', and '''Joseph Morales'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn November 1, [[USADA]] announced a finding of no-fault after Moreno failed a drug test. He tested positive for trace amounts of the banned substance [[clenbuterol]] in an in-competition test in the early hours of the event. But after an investigation, USADA determined that the positive likely came from contaminated meat, an issue well known to the anti-doping agency.{{Cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/usada-ufcs-brandon-moreno-accepts-no-fault-finding-after-failed-drug-test|title=USADA: UFC's Brandon Moreno accepts no-fault finding after failed drug test|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2017-11-01|access-date=2017-11-01}}\n\nWith seven first-round finishes, this event ties the record for most first-round finishes in a single UFC event.{{Cite web |date=2017-08-06 |title=Relive all 7 record-tying 1st-round finishes from UFC Fight Night 114 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2017/08/relive-all-7-record-tying-1st-round-finishes-from-ufc-fight-night-114 |access-date=2022-11-13 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List Fight Night: Saint Preux vs. Okami]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Cowboy vs. Till]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 216: Ferguson vs. Lee''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on October 7, 2017, at [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas metropolitan area]].{{cite news|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/6/26/15875518/report-ufc-late-2017-schedule-ppv-detroit-return-msg-nyc-november-mma-news|title=UFC's late 2017 schedule includes PPV in Detroit, return to MSG in November|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2017-06-26|access-date=2017-06-26|archive-date=2023-07-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230707162211/https://bloodyelbow.com/2017/6/26/15875518/report-ufc-late-2017-schedule-ppv-detroit-return-msg-nyc-november-mma-news|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Stipe Miocic]] and former two-time champion [[Cain Velasquez]] was initially targeted to headline this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/ufc-werkt-aan-stipe-miocic-vs-cain-velasquez-voor-ufc-216/|title=UFC works on Stipe Miocic vs. Cain Velasquez for UFC 216|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Lennart Van Arendonk|date=2017-07-18|access-date=2017-07-18|language=nl}} After that pairing failed to materialize, the promotion announced plans for an interim [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]] welterweight winner [[Tony Ferguson]] and [[Kevin Lee]] to serve as the main event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/tony-ferguson-kevin-lee-ufc-216-interim-lightweight-title|title=Tony Ferguson, Kevin Lee will fight at UFC 216 for interim lightweight title|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco and John Morgan|date=2017-08-15|access-date=2017-08-15}}{{cite news|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/8/29/16223696/mma-news-tony-ferguson-vs-kevin-lee-ufc-216-main-event|title=Tony Ferguson vs. Kevin Lee officially announced as UFC 216 main event|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2017-08-29|access-date=2017-08-29|archive-date=2019-02-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190204174421/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/8/29/16223696/mma-news-tony-ferguson-vs-kevin-lee-ufc-216-main-event|url-status=dead}}\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Demetrious Johnson]] and [[Ray Borg]] was expected to headline [[UFC 215]].{{cite news|url=https://www.tsn.ca/two-title-fights-headline-ufc-215-in-edmonton-1.809726|title=Two title fights headline UFC 215 in Edmonton|publisher=tsn.ca|author=Staff|date=2017-07-20|access-date=2017-09-13}} However, the fight was canceled a day before the event, as Borg was forced to withdraw from the fight due to illness. His illness was unrelated to his weight cut, sources say.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/8/16272540/demetrious-johnson-vs-ray-borg-ufc-215-main-event-canceled|title=Demetrious Johnson vs. Ray Borg UFC 215 main event canceled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-09-08|access-date=2017-09-13}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event. If successful, Johnson would have the all-time most consecutive UFC title defenses in any division, with eleven.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/13/16303874/demetrious-johnson-vs-ray-borg-rebooked-for-ufc-216-fight-card|title=Demetrious Johnson vs. Ray Borg rebooked for UFC 216|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2017-09-13|access-date=2017-09-13}}\n\n[[Abel Trujillo]] was expected to face [[Lando Vannata]] at the event. However, Trujillo was removed from the card on August 14 for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Bobby Green]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Abel-Trujillo-Pulled-Lando-Vannata-Now-Faces-Bobby-Green-at-UFC-216-on-Oct-7-in-Las-Vegas-126093|title=Abel Trujillo pulled; Lando Vannata now faces Bobby Green at UFC 216 on Oct. 7 in Las Vegas|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jordan Breen|date=2017-08-14|access-date=2017-08-14}}\n\n[[Paige VanZant]] was expected to face [[Jessica Eye]] at the event. However, VanZant pulled out of the fight on September 25 citing a back injury and the bout was scrapped.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/25/16361370/paige-vanzant-vs-jessica-eye-expected-to-be-pulled-from-ufc-216|title=Paige VanZant vs. Jessica Eye pulled from UFC 216|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-09-25|access-date=2017-09-25}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between promotional newcomers [[Andrea Lee (fighter)|Andrea Lee]] and [[Kalindra Faria]] was expected to be a late addition to this event, as it was announced on September 28.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/andrea-lee-kalindra-faria-late-addition-ufc-216|title=Andrea Lee vs. Kalindra Faria a late addition to next week's UFC 216 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-09-28|access-date=2017-09-28}} However a few hours later, it was revealed that Lee was pulled from the contest as she did not go through the [[USADA]] testing pool for six months. Usually short notice fights do not need to follow such rule, but since she has failed a drug test in the past, Lee must fulfill that requirement.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/28/16381220/andrea-lee-pulled-from-ufc-216-fight-kalindra-faria|title=Andrea Lee pulled from UFC 216 fight with Kalindra Faria|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2017-09-28|access-date=2017-09-28}} She was replaced by fellow promotional newcomer [[Mara Romero Borella]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/kalindra-faria-mara-romero-borella-ufc-216-main-card|title=Kalindra Faria remains on Saturday's UFC 216 fight card with main-card slot|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-02|access-date=2017-10-03}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Nik Lentz]] and former [[List of Bellator champions#Lightweight Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]] [[Will Brooks]] was slated for the preliminary portion of the card. However the pairing was scrapped during the weigh-ins for the event, as Lentz was stricken with \"medical issues\" and deemed unfit to compete.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ufc-216-loses-featured-prelim-will-brook-nik-lentz|title=UFC 216 loses a featured prelim on eve of event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-06|access-date=2017-10-06}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between former heavyweight champion [[Fabrício Werdum]] and [[Derrick Lewis]] was slated for the main card. However, the fight was scrapped the day of the event when Lewis could not move due to back pain from a previous back injury. As a result, [[Walt Harris (fighter)|Walt Harris]], who was originally scheduled to face [[Mark Godbeer]] in a preliminary bout, was tabbed as a short-notice opponent for Werdum.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/derrick-lewis-injured-ufc-216-fight-vs-fabricio-werdum-cancelled-last-minute|title=Derrick Lewis injured; Fabricio Werdum now faces Walt Harris last minute at UFC 216|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Simon Samano and John Morgan|date=2017-10-07|access-date=2017-10-07}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent fighters were awarded $50,000 bonuses:{{Cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ufc-216-bonuses-demetrious-johnsons-late-finish-became-his-eighth-ufc-fight-night-bonuss|title=UFC 216 bonuses: reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $2,178,000.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/full-ufc-216-salaries-tony-ferguson-payroll-2-1-million|title=Full UFC 216 salaries: New champ Tony Ferguson gets $500K of event's $2.2-million payout (Updated)|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=2017-10-09|access-date=2017-10-09}} \n* '''Tony Ferguson:''' $500,000 (includes $250,000 win bonus) def. Kevin Lee: $250,000\n* '''Demetrious Johnson:''' $370,000 (no win bonus) def. Ray Borg: $100,000\n* '''Fabrício Werdum:''' $400,000 (includes $125,000 win bonus) def. Walt Harris: $28,000\n* '''Mara Romero Borella:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Kalindra Faria: $12,000\n* Beneil Dariush: $48,000 drew with Evan Dunham: $40,000\n* '''Cody Stamann:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Tom Duquesnoy: $23,000\n* Lando Vannata: $25,000 drew with Bobby Green: $24,000\n* '''Poliana Botelho:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Pearl Gonzalez: $10,000\n* '''Matt Schnell:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Marco Beltrán: $14,000\n* '''John Moraga:''' $68,000 (includes $34,000 win bonus) def. Magomed Bibulatov: $17,000\n* '''Brad Tavares:''' $74,000 (includes $37,000 win bonus) def. Thales Leites: $57,000\n\n''Although not included on the NSAC's initial report, Mark Godbeer ($15,000/$15,000) was paid his show purse and a win bonus after he was removed from his scheduled bout with Walt Harris on the day of the event.''\n\n==Records set==\nWith his win, Johnson broke [[Anderson Silva]]'s record for most consecutive title defenses (11).{{cite Shevchenko 2]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Volkov vs. Struve''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 115''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the Ultimate Fighting Championship held on September 2, 2017 at [[Rotterdam Ahoy]] in [[Rotterdam]], [[Netherlands]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/ufc-announces-2017-events-in-scotland-netherlands-and-poland|title=UFC announces 2017 events in Scotland, Netherlands and Poland|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=2017-03-16|access-date=2017-05-28}}\n\n==Background==\nA heavyweight bout between former [[List of Bellator champions#Heavyweight Championship|Bellator Heavyweight Champion]] [[Alexander Volkov (fighter)|Alexander Volkov]] and [[Stefan Struve]] headlined the event.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Struve-and-Volkov-Headline-UFC-Return-to-the-Netherlands|title=Struve and Volkov headline UFC's Fight return to the Netherlands|publisher=ufc.com|author=press release|date=2017-05-27|access-date=2017-05-27}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/stefan-struve-vs-alexander-volkov-headlines-septembers-ufc-fight-night-in-rotterdam|title=Stefan Struve vs. Alexander Volkov headlines September's UFC Fight Night in Rotterdam|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-27|access-date=2017-05-27}}\n\nPromotional newcomer Abu Azaitar was expected to face [[Siyar Bahadurzada]] at the event, but pulled out in early August and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Rob Wilkinson]].{{cite news|url=http://www.fightnewsaustralia.com/newly-signed-rob-wilkinson-to-face-siyar-bahadurzada-at-ufc-fight-night-115/|title=Newly signed Rob Wilkinson to face Siyar Bahadurzada at UFC Fight Night 115|publisher=fightnewsaustralia.com|author=C. Post|date=2017-07-30|access-date=2017-08-08}}\n\nAlso in early August, [[Islam Makhachev]] pulled out of a scheduled bout against [[Michel Prazeres]] and was replaced by another newcomer in [[Mads Burnell]].{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/islam-makhachev-out-mads-burnell-maakt-ufc-debuut-in-rotterdam-tegen-michel-prazeres/|title=Islam Makhachev out, Mads Burnell makes UFC debuut in Rotterdam against Michel Prazeres|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-08-05|access-date=2017-08-08|language=nl}}\n\nOn August 11, [[Marcos Rogério de Lima]] was pulled from his bout against [[Saparbek Safarov]] after being flagged by the U.S. Anti-Doping Agency ([[USADA]]) for a potential anti-doping violation stemming from an out-of-competition sample collected on August 1.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/8/11/16137130/marcos-rogerio-de-lima-flagged-for-potential-usada-violation-pulled-from-ufc-fight-night-115|title=Marcos Rogerio de Lima flagged for potential USADA violation, pulled from UFC Fight Night 115|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2017-08-11|access-date=2017-08-12}} Subsequently, Safarov was also forced from the card with a rib injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/ook-saparbek-safarov-vecht-niet-tijdens-ufc-rotterdam/|title=Saparbek Safarov will not fight during UFC Rotterdam|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-08-16|access-date=2017-08-16|language=nl}}\n\n[[Nick Hein]] was expected to face promotional newcomer [[Zabit Magomedsharipov]] at this event, but pulled out on August 21 due to injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/nick-hein-ufc-rotterdam-featherweight-zabit-magomedsharipov|title=Nick Hein out at UFC-Rotterdam, promotion seeks featherweight foe for Zabit Magomedsharipov|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-08-21|access-date=2017-08-22}} Hein was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Mike Santiago (fighter)|Mike Santiago]] .{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/mike-santiago-zabit-magomedsharipov-ufc-rotterdam|title=DWCS 7 winner Mike Santiago meets Zabit Magomedsharipov at UFC-Rotterdam|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-08-28|access-date=2017-08-28}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]] [[Germaine de Randamie]] was expected to face [[Marion Reneau]] at the event. However, de Randamie pulled out of the fight on August 25 citing injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/germaine-de-randamie-out-ufc-fight-night-115|title=Germaine de Randamie out at UFC Fight Night 115, promotion seeking replacement|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-08-25|access-date=2017-08-25}} She was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Talita Bernardo]].{{cite news|url=http://mmabrasil.com.br/talita-de-oliveira-substitui-de-randamie-e-encara-marion-reneau-no-ufc-fight-night-115|title=Talita de Oliveira replaces De Randamie and faces Marion Reneau at UFC Fight Night 115|publisher=mmabrasil.com.br|author=Gabriel Carvalho|date=2017-08-28|access-date=2017-08-28|language=pt|archive-date=2018-10-29|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181029200420/http://mmabrasil.com.br/talita-de-oliveira-substitui-de-randamie-e-encara-marion-reneau-no-ufc-fight-night-115|url-status=dead}}\n\nAt the weigh ins, [[Michel Prazeres]] missed the lightweight limit of 156 pounds, coming in at 159 pounds. As a result his bout with Mads Burnell was changed to a catchweight and Prazeres was fined 20% of his purse.{{Cite news|url=http://mmauno.com/one-fighter-misses-weight-3lbs-ufc-rotterdam/|title=One fighter misses weight by 3-pounds at UFC Rotterdam|publisher=mmauno.com|author=Jim Edwards|date=2017-09-01|access-date=2017-09-03}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (UFC Fight [[Alberta]], [[Canada]]\n|attendance=16,232{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/ufc-215-draws-announced-sellout-of-16232-in-edmonton-debut-despite-losing-johnson-borg|title=UFC 215 draws Fight Night: Volkov vs. Struve]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Rockhold vs. Branch]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 215: Nunes vs. Shevchenko 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on September 9, 2017, at [[Rogers Place]] in [[Edmonton, Alberta]], [[Canada]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/04/ufc-216-slated-for-sept-9-in-edmonton|title=UFC 216 slated for Sept. 9 in Edmonton|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-04-27|access-date=2017-04-27}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/with-aug-19-event-scrapped-edmonton-now-hosts-ufc-215-on-sept-9|title=With Aug. 19 event scrapped, Edmonton now hosts UFC 215 on Sept. 9|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-06-26|access-date=2017-06-26}}\n\n==Background==\nThe promotion was initially planning a PPV event for August 19 at a venue to be in announced in the [[United States]] (presumably [[Seattle]]), possibly with [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] and Seattle-area resident [[Demetrious Johnson]]. However, those plans were nixed on June 26, though no exact reason was given. Therefore, an originally scheduled UFC 216 to be held at [[Rogers Place]] in [[Edmonton, Alberta]], [[Canada]], on September 9 was renamed as UFC 215.\n\nWhile the UFC has hosted many events across Canada, the event was the first held in Edmonton (second in Alberta, after [[2012 in UFC#UFC 149: Faber vs. Barão|UFC 149]] in 2012). [[Zuffa]] hosted a [[World Extreme Cagefighting]] event, [[WEC 49]], at [[Rexall Place]] in Edmonton in June 2010.\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Demetrious Johnson]] and [[Ray Borg]] was expected to serve as the event headliner. If successful in his title defense, Johnson would have become the UFC fighter with most title defenses of all time, with a total of eleven.{{cite news|url=https://www.tsn.ca/two-title-fights-headline-ufc-215-in-edmonton-1.809726|title=Two title fights headline UFC 215 in Edmonton|publisher=tsn.ca|author=Staff|date=2017-07-20|access-date=2017-07-20}} In turn, the fight was canceled a day before the event, as Borg was forced to withdraw from the fight on Thursday evening due to illness. According to multiple sources, Borg was battling an illness and was deemed unfit to fight by UFC doctors. His illness was unrelated to his weight cut, sources say.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/8/16272540/demetrious-johnson-vs-ray-borg-ufc-215-main-event-canceled|title=Demetrious Johnson vs. Ray Borg UFC 215 main event canceled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-09-08|access-date=2017-09-08}} The fight was later rescheduled for [[UFC 216]] the following month.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/13/16303874/demetrious-johnson-vs-ray-borg-rebooked-for-ufc-216-fight-card|title=Demetrious Johnson vs. Ray Borg rebooked for UFC 216|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2017-09-13|access-date=2017-09-13}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] and multiple-time [[Muay Thai]] world champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] took place at this event.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Debuts-in-Edmonton-with-Two-Thrilling-World-Championship-Fights|title=UFC debuts in Edmonton with two thrilling world championship fights|publisher=ufc.com|author=Press Release|date=2017-07-20|access-date=2017-07-20}} The pairing met previously in March 2016 at [[UFC 196]], with Nunes winning by unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/ufc-196-results-amanda-nunes-outlasts-valentina-shevchenko-for-decision-win|title=UFC 196 results: Amanda Nunes outlasts Valentina Shevchenko for decision win|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Ben Fowlkes|date=2016-03-05|access-date=2017-05-04}} The pairing was initially expected to meet again at [[UFC 213]].{{cite web|url=http://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-213-amanda-nunes-vs-valentina-shevchenko-title-fight-set-for-july/|title=UFC 213: Amanda Nunes vs. Valentina Shevchenko title fight set for July|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brandon Wise|date=2017-05-12|access-date=2017-05-12}} However, the bout was canceled just hours before the event started as Nunes pulled out, citing an illness despite being medically cleared to fight.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/7/8/15941740/amanda-nunes-ill-out-of-ufc-213-main-event|title=Amanda Nunes ill, out of UFC 213 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-07-08|access-date=2017-07-08}} Due to the cancelation of the Johnson-Borg bout, this fight acted as the new main event.\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Sara McMann]] and [[Ketlen Vieira]] was expected to take place at [[UFC 214]], but on July 7 it was announced that the bout was moved to this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/7/7/15939564/sara-mcmann-vs-ketlen-vieira-moved-to-ufc-215-edmonton|title=Sara McMann vs. Ketlen Vieira moved to UFC 215 in Edmonton|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-07|access-date=2017-07-07}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Junior dos Santos]] was expected to face [[Francis Ngannou]], but on August 18 it was announced that he was pulled from the card after being notified by [[USADA]] of a potential doping violation. He was flagged after an out-of-competition drug test conducted August 10.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/junior-dos-santos-potential-doping-violation-pulled-ufc-215|title=Junior Dos Santos notified of potential doping violation, pulled from UFC 215|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-08-18|access-date=2017-08-21}} Dos Santos tested positive for [[hydrochlorothiazide]] and his team is currently working on testing supplements as a possible source for it.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/junior-dos-santos-rep-team-wants-2-supplements-tested-for-source-of-hydrochlorothiazide|title=Junior Dos Santos rep: Team wants 2 supplements tested for source of Hydrochlorothiazide|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-08-20|access-date=2017-08-21}} In turn, Ngannou was removed from the card after promotion officials deemed that a suitable opponent could not be arranged.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/8/28/16218846/francis-ngannou-is-officially-off-ufc-215|title=Francis Ngannou is officially off UFC 215|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2017-08-28|access-date=2017-08-28}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/demetrious-johnson-amanda-nunes-ufc-215-lineup-set-edmonton|title=UFC 215 lineup set with 2 title fights in Edmonton, including Demetrious Johnson's quest for record|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-08-28|access-date=2017-08-28}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Bantamweight\n|[[Amanda Fight Night: Brunson vs. Machida]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Poirier vs. Pettis]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 217: Bisping vs. St-Pierre''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on November 4, 2017, at [[Madison Square Garden]] in [[New York City]], [[New York (state)|New York]].{{cite news|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/6/26/15875518/report-ufc-late-2017-schedule-ppv-detroit-return-msg-nyc-november-mma-news|title=UFC's late 2017 schedule includes PPV in Detroit, return to MSG in November|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2017-06-26|access-date=2017-06-26|archive-date=2023-07-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230707162211/https://bloodyelbow.com/2017/6/26/15875518/report-ufc-late-2017-schedule-ppv-detroit-return-msg-nyc-november-mma-news|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\n{{multiple image\n| align = left\n| direction = \n| width = \n| total_width = 330\n| image1 = Michael Bisping.jpg\n| width1 = 2313\n| height1 = 2600\n| alt1 = \n| caption1 = \n| image2 = Georges St-Pierre crop.jpg\n| width2 = 2504\n| height2 = 2600\n| alt2 = \n| caption2 = \n| footer = St-Pierre ''(right)'' fought for the first time in four years, as he tried to become the fourth fighter to win titles in different weight classes in the UFC. Meanwhile, Michael Bisping ''(left)'' had his 2nd title defense since he became the champion at [[UFC 199]] in June 2016.\n}}\n\nThe event was the second that the promotion has hosted at Madison Square Garden.\n\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between current champion and [[The Ultimate Fighter 3]] light heavyweight winner [[Michael Bisping]] and former two-time [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Georges St-Pierre]] headlined this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/champ-michael-bisping-says-georges-st-pierre-title-bout-official-for-ufc-217-in-nyc|title=Champ Michael Bisping says Georges St-Pierre title bout official for UFC 217 in NYC|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-08-02|access-date=2017-08-02}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/ufc-217-official-title-fights-michael-bisping-georges-st-pierre-cody-garbrandt-tj-dillashaw|title=Bisping-GSP, Garbrandt-Dillashaw title fights among 7 official for UFC 217|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-08-16|access-date=2017-08-16}} The bout was originally announced in March, but without a date attached. As time wore on the contest appeared to fall apart, with UFC President [[Dana White]] claiming Bisping would fight interim middleweight champion [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] next and St-Pierre would get current welterweight champion [[Tyron Woodley]]. When Whittaker announced he was injured and Woodley put on a successful title defense against [[Demian Maia]] at [[UFC 214]] in July that didn't meet White's standards, he reverted to the initial plan, which St-Pierre and Bisping said they wanted all along.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/georges-st-pierre-signs-bout-agreement-michael-bisping-ufc-217|title=Georges St-Pierre officially signs agreement to fight Michael Bisping at UFC 217|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-08-09|access-date=2017-08-09}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between champion [[Cody Garbrandt]] and former champion [[T.J. Dillashaw]], was previously scheduled to serve as headliner for [[UFC 213]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/04/dana-white-confirms-bantamweight-champ-cody-garbrandt-meets-former-champ-t-j-dillashaw-at-ufc-213|title=Dana White confirms bantamweight champ Cody Garbrandt meets former champ T.J. Dillashaw at UFC 213|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-04-09|access-date=2017-08-09}} However, Garbrandt withdrew from the fight due to a back injury.{{cite web|url=https://bleacherreport.com/articles/2710774-tj-dillashaw-vs-cody-garbrandt-scratched-from-ufc-213|title=TJ Dillashaw vs. Cody Garbrandt scratched from UFC 213|publisher=bleacherreport.com|author=Rob Goldberg|date=2017-05-23|access-date=2017-08-09}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/cody-garbrandt-vs-tj-dillashaw-herboekt-voor-ufc-217-in-new-york/|title=Cody Garbrandt vs. TJ Dillashaw rebooked for UFC 217 in New York|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-08-09|access-date=2017-08-09|language=nl}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/cody-garbrandt-t-j-dillashaw-ufc-217-madison-square-garden-new-york|title=Champ Cody Garbrandt says he's fighting T.J. Dillashaw at UFC 217 at Madison Square Garden|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-08-09|access-date=2017-08-09}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between champion [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] and former title challenger [[Rose Namajunas]] took place as the third title fight of the event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/ufc-217-3rd-title-fight-champ-joanna-jedrzejczyk-rose-namajunas|title=UFC 217 gets 3rd title fight: champ Joanna Jedrzejczyk vs. Rose Namajunas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=2017-09-12|access-date=2017-09-12}}\n\n[[Gadzhimurad Antigulov]] was expected to face [[Ion Cuțelaba]] at the event. However, Antigulov pulled out of the fight on September 26 citing an injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/gadzhimurad-antigulov-geblesseerd-gevecht-tegen-ion-cutelaba-gecanceld/|title=Gadzhimurad Antigulov injured, fight against Ion Cutelaba canceled|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-09-26|access-date=2017-09-26|language=nl}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Michał Oleksiejczuk]].{{cite web|url=http://www.lowking.pl/michal-oleksiejczuk-ufc-powalczy-ionem-cutelaba-ufc-217/|title=Michał Oleksiejczuk at the UFC – will fight with Ion Cutelaba at UFC 217|publisher=lowking.pl|author=Bartłomiej Stachura|date=2017-10-13|access-date=2017-10-13|language=pl}} In turn after the weigh-ins, Cuțelaba was pulled from the event by [[USADA]] due to a potential Anti-Doping Policy violation stemming from its investigation into voluntary disclosures by Cuțelaba during an out-of-competition sample collections on October 18 and October 19. He was provisionally suspended and the bout cancelled.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/ion-cutelaba-possible-usada-violation-removed-ufc-217|title=Ion Cutelaba flagged for possible USADA violation, removed from UFC 217|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2017-11-03|access-date=2017-11-04}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Walt Harris (fighter)|Walt Harris]] and [[Mark Godbeer]] was initially slated for [[UFC 216]]. However, the fight was scrapped on the day of the event as Harris, was tabbed replacement opponent for [[Fabrício Werdum]] after his scheduled opponent [[Derrick Lewis]] pulled out of their fight with a back injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/derrick-lewis-injured-ufc-216-fight-vs-fabricio-werdum-cancelled-last-minute|title=Derrick Lewis injured; Fabricio Werdum now faces Walt Harris last minute at UFC 216|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Simon Samano and John Morgan|date=2017-10-07|access-date=2017-10-07}} The Harris/Godbeer pairing was rescheduled and took place at this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmauno.com/mark-godbeer-and-walt-harris-offered-to-reschedule-bout-at-ufc-217/|title=Mark Godbeer and Walt Harris reschedule bout at UFC 217|publisher=mmauno.com|author=Jim Edwards|date=2017-10-09|access-date=2017-10-09}}\n\n[[Patrick Cummins (fighter)|Patrick Cummins]] was expected to face [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Edgar vs. Team Penn]] light heavyweight winner [[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]] at this event. However, on October 17, Cummins pulled out due to a resistant [[staph infection]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/patrick-cummins-ufc-217-fight-card-mutant-staph-infection|title=Patrick Cummins says he's off UFC 217 fight card due to 'mutant staph infection'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-17|access-date=2017-10-17}} He was replaced by former interim [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Ovince Saint Preux]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ovince-saint-preux-replaces-patrick-cummins-corey-anderson-ufc-217|title=Ovince Saint Preux replaces Patrick Cummins, meets Corey Anderson at UFC 217 in NYC|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-10-19|access-date=2017-10-19}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Georges St-Pierre]]\n|def.\n|[[Michael Bisping]] (c)\n|Technical Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|3\n|4:23\n|For the [[UFC Middleweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[T.J. produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on December 2, 2017, at [[Little Caesars Arena]] in [[Detroit|Detroit, Michigan]].{{cite news|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/6/26/15875518/report-ufc-late-2017-schedule-ppv-detroit-return-msg-nyc-november-mma-news|title=UFC's late 2017 schedule includes PPV in Detroit, return to MSG in November|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2017-06-26|access-date=2017-06-26|archive-date=2023-07-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230707162211/https://bloodyelbow.com/2017/6/26/15875518/report-ufc-late-2017-schedule-ppv-detroit-return-msg-nyc-november-mma-news|url-status=dead}}{{cite news|url=http://www.freep.com/story/sports/2017/07/30/ufc-218-detroit-little-caesars-arena/522998001/|title=Little Caesars Arena to host UFC 218 in December|publisher=freep.com|author=Christopher D. Thomas|date=2017-07-30|access-date=2017-07-30}}\n\n==Background==\nAfter previously contesting two events in the [[Metro Detroit]] area (and the first since [[UFC 123]] in November 2010), the event was the first for the promotion at the newly built venue.\n\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Max Holloway]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Frankie Edgar]] was expected to serve as the main event.{{cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/20909077/max-holloway-face-frankie-edgar-featherweight-title-bout-ufc-218|title=Max Holloway to put featherweight title up against Frankie Edgar|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2017-10-03|access-date=2017-10-03}} However, on November 11, Edgar was forced to withdraw from the fight citing injury. He was replaced by former [[List of WEC champions#Featherweight Championship|WEC Featherweight Champion]] and former two-time champion [[José Aldo]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/11/11/16633192/max-holloway-vs-jose-aldo-2-slated-as-new-ufc-218-main-event|title=Max Holloway vs. Jose Aldo 2 slated as new UFC 218 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-11-11|access-date=2017-11-11}} Aldo was scheduled to face [[Ricardo Lamas]] at [[UFC on Fox: Lawler vs. dos Anjos]] in a rematch of his successful title defense at [[UFC 169]] in February, 2014.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/jose-aldo-vs-ricardo-lamas-rematch-co-headlines-ufc-on-fox-26-perry-ponzinibbio-mein-silva-added|title=Jose Aldo vs. Ricardo Lamas rematch co-headlines UFC on FOX 26; Perry-Ponzinibbio, Mein-Silva added|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-13|access-date=2017-11-11}} Holloway and Aldo met previously at [[UFC 212]], with the former defeating the latter to win the championship.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/6/4/15736310/ufc-212-results-max-holloway-finishes-jose-aldo-claims-featherweight-crown|title=UFC 212 results: Max Holloway finishes José Aldo, claims featherweight crown|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2017-06-04|access-date=2017-11-11}}\n\nA [[List of UFC Champions#Heavyweight championship|UFC Heavyweight title eliminator]] bout between former [[K-1 Grand Prix]] champion [[Alistair Overeem]] and up and coming contender [[Francis Ngannou]] co-headlined the event. The winner would be matched against champion [[Stipe Miocic]] for the title. Miocic had previously defeated Overeem by first round [[knockout]] at [[UFC 203]] in September 2016.\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 55 kg|2008 Olympic gold medalist in freestyle wrestling]] and former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Henry Cejudo]] and [[Sergio Pettis]], initially scheduled for [[UFC 211]] before Cejudo pulled out with a hand injury, was rescheduled for this event.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/27/16376430/henry-cejudo-vs-sergio-pettis-targeted-for-ufc-218|title=Henry Cejudo vs. Sergio Pettis targeted for UFC 218|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-09-27|access-date=2017-09-27}} Subsequently, Cejudo's availability for the event was briefly in doubt as he suffered minor burn injuries to his right foot on the evening of October 9 as he was evacuating his hotel as a result of the [[Tubbs Fire]] near [[Santa Rosa, California]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/10/10/16453918/henry-cejudo-suffers-burns-loses-gold-medal-in-california-wildfire|title=Henry Cejudo suffers burns, loses gold medal in California wildfire|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-10-10|access-date=2017-10-10}} The following day, his management team confirmed that the fight with Pettis was still on for the event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/henry-cejudo-sergio-pettis-ufc-218-injuries-lost-olympic-medal-fire|title=Manager: Henry Cejudo will fight Sergio Pettis at UFC 218 despite injuries, lost Olympic medal in fire|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-10-10|access-date=2017-10-10}}\n\n[[Al Iaquinta]] was scheduled to face [[Paul Felder]] at the event. However, Iaquinta pulled out of the fight on October 31 citing an injury.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/10/31/16586154/ufc-218-loses-paul-felder-vs-al-iaquinta|title=UFC 218 loses Paul Felder vs. Al Iaquinta|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-10-31|access-date=2017-10-31}} Felder remained on the card against [[Charles Oliveira]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/al-iaquinta-out-charles-oliveira-in-versus-paul-felder-ufc-218|title=Al Iaquinta out, Charles Oliveira in against Paul Felder at UFC 218|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2017-10-31|access-date=2017-10-31}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Featherweight|[[Max Holloway]] Branch]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 216|UFC 216: Ferguson vs. Lee]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Saint Preux vs. Okami''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 117''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on September 23, 2017, at [[Saitama Super Arena]] in [[Saitama, Saitama|Saitama]], [[Japan]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Set-to-Return-to-Japan-for-Fight-Night-Event-on-Sept-23-121501|title=UFC set to return to Japan for Fight Night event on Sept. 23|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2017-05-16|access-date=2017-07-05}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the fifth that the organization has hosted in Saitama, and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Barnett vs. Nelson]] in September 2015.\n\nA light heavyweight rematch between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Maurício Rua]] and former interim title challenger [[Ovince Saint Preux]] was expected to headline this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Shogun-Saint-Preux-UFC-Japan-2017|title=Former Pride legend Shogun Rua to headline return to Saitama Super Arena|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2017-07-06|access-date=2017-07-06}} The pairing met previously at [[UFC Fight Night: Shogun vs. Saint Preux]] in November 2014 where Saint Preux defeated Rua via knockout in the fight's opening minute.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/11/ufc-fight-night-56-results-ovince-saint-preux-destroys-mauricio-rua-in-34-seconds|title=UFC Fight Night 56 results: Ovince Saint Preux destroys Mauricio Rua in 34 seconds|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2014-11-09|access-date=2017-07-06}} On September 16, Rua withdrew from the fight due to a knee injury and was replaced by returning veteran, former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Yushin Okami]].{{Cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/shogun-rua-out-yushin-okami-in-to-face-ovince-saint-preux-in-ufc-japan-headliner|title='Shogun' Rua out, Yushin Okami in to face Ovince Saint Preux in UFC-Japan headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Ben Fowlkes|date=2017-09-16|access-date=2017-09-18}}\n\n[[Naoki Inoue]] was expected to face [[Jenel Lausa]] at the event, but pulled out on September 9 due to a dislocated shoulder. As a result, Lausa was removed from the card and is expected to be rescheduled against a new opponent at a future event.{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/naoki-inoue-vs-jenel-lausa-geschrapt-voor-ufc-fight-night-117-in-saitama|title=Naoki Inoue vs. Jenel Lausa scrapped from UFC Fight Night 117 in Saitama|author=Marcel Dorff|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2017-09-09|access-date=2017-09-09|language=nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Mizuto Hirota]] missed the featherweight limit of 146 pounds, coming in at 150 pounds. As a result, his bout with [[Charles Rosa]] was changed to a catchweight and the fight was initially expected to proceed as scheduled. Subsequently, Hirota was fined 30% of his purse.{{Cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/mizuto-hirota-missed-weight-ufc-japan-dazed-stumbled-150-pounds|title=Mizuto Hirota missed weight for UFC-Japan – and it was pretty scary|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-09-21|access-date=2017-09-21}} Later that day, Hirota was pulled due to health and safety concerns, as he was deemed unfit to compete by the UFC medical team.{{Cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/mizuto-hirota-pulled-ufc-japan-scary-weigh-in-incident|title=Mizuto Hirota pulled from UFC-Japan after scary event was the third that the UFC has hosted in [[Pittsburgh, Pennsylvania]].\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Luke Rockhold]] and former [[WSOF Middleweight Championship|WSOF Middleweight]] and [[WSOF Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[David Branch (fighter)|David Branch]] headlined this event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/luke-rockhold-returns-ufc-fight-night-116-headliner-vs-david-branch|title=Ex-champ Luke Rockhold returns vs. David Branch in September's UFC Fight Night 116 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-07-22|access-date=2017-07-23}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Justin Ledet]] and Dmitriy Sosnovskiy was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Bermudez vs. The Korean Zombie]]. However, Ledet pulled out of the fight on January 26 alleging an undisclosed injury, and promotion officials elected to remove Sosnovskiy from the card.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmanews.com/report-justin-ledet-off-ufc-fight-night-card-in-houston-with-an-injury/|title=Justin Ledet off UFC Fight Night card in Houston with an substance.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/4/5/15192890/ufc-heavyweight-justin-ledet-accepts-four-month-usada-suspension|title=UFC heavyweight Justin Ledet accepts four-month USADA newcomer Zu Anyanwu.{{cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/zu-anywanwu-ufc-pittsburgh/|title=Zu Anyanwu gets long awaited call to UFC, fights Saturday in Pittsburgh|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Eric Perry]] and former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Thiago Alves (fighter)|Thiago Alves]], as well as a middleweight bout between former [[Bellator Middleweight Championship|Bellator Middleweight Champion]] [[Héctor Lombard]] and [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]].{{cite Pittsburgh|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-07-19|access-date=2017-07-23}} In turn, Alves pulled out of the fight on September 13.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/thiago-alves-off-ufc-pittsburgh-replacement-sought-mike-perry|title=Thiago Alves out of Saturday's UFC co-headliner; replacement foe sought for Mike news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/13/16304446/with-thiago-alves-out-mike-perry-meets-newcomer-alex-reyes-at-ufc-pittsburgh|title=With Thiago Alves out, Mike Perry meets newcomer Alex Reyes at UFC Pittsburgh|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2017-09-13|access-date=2017-09-13}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Krzysztof Jotko]] and [[Uriah Hall]] was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Jędrzejczyk vs. Penne]] in June 2015.
