vs. Machida]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Cowboy vs. Till''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 118''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on October 21, 2017 at [[Ergo Arena]] in [[Gdańsk]], [[Poland]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/ufc-announces-2017-events-in-scotland-netherlands-and-poland|title=UFC announces 2017 events in Scotland, Netherlands and Poland|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=2017-03-16|access-date=2017-03-16}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the first that the UFC has hosted in Gdańsk and the second in Poland, after [[UFC Fight Night: Gonzaga vs. Cro Cop 2]] in April 2015.\n\nThe promotion was initially targeting a middleweight bout between [[Thiago Santos (fighter)|Thiago Santos]] and promotional newcomer [[Michał Materla]] to headline the event.{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/zeer-verrassend-main-event-tussen-thiago-santos-en-michal-materla-tijdens-ufc-gdansk/|title=Very surprising Main Event between Thiago Santos and Michal Materla during UFC Gdansk |publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-08-29|access-date=2017-08-29|language=nl}} However it was revealed on 1 September that Materla had reneged on his agreement with the UFC and was re-signing with the Polish regional promotion [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki|KSW]].{{cite news|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2017/09/materla-desiste-de-luta-contra-thiago-marreta-e-renova-contrato-com-o-ksw.html|title=Materla gives up fight against Thiago Marreta and renews contract with KSW |publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2017-09-01|access-date=2017-09-01|language=pt}} In turn, Santos was removed from the card and faced [[Jack Hermansson]] a week later at [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Machida]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/michal-materla-jack-hermansson-thiago-marreta-santos-ufc-fight-night-119-brazil|title=Michał Materla out, Jack Hermansson in at UFC Fight Night 119 in Brazil|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=2017-09-04|access-date=2017-09-04}}\n\nSubsequently, a welterweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger [[Donald Cerrone]] and [[Darren Till]] was tabbed as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://mmauno.com/donald-cerrone-vs-darren-till-works-ufc-gdansk/|title=Donald Cerrone vs Darren Till in the works for UFC Gdansk|publisher=mmauno.com|author=Jim Edwards|date=2017-09-07|access-date=2017-09-07}}{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/7/16267620/donald-cerrone-darren-till-headlines-ufc-gdansk-poland|title=Donald Cerrone vs. Darren Till slated to headline UFC Gdansk|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2017-09-07|access-date=2017-09-07}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Dmitry Smolyakov]] and promotional newcomer [[Adam Wieczorek]] was initially scheduled to take place at [[UFC 214]]. However, the bout was removed from that card for undisclosed reasons on July 17 and was expected take place at this event.{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/ufc-gdansk-kent-met-adam-wieczorek-vs-dmitrii-smoliakov-eerste-gevecht/|title=UFC Gdansk knows first fight with Adam Wieczorek vs. Dmitrii Smoliakov|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-07-18|access-date=2017-07-19|language=nl}} In turn, Smolyakov was removed from the event on October 4 and was replaced by [[Anthony Hamilton (fighter)|Anthony Hamilton]].{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/anthony-hamilton-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-adam-wieczorek-tijdens-ufc-gdansk/|title=Anthony Hamilton takes a short notice against Adam Wieczorek during UFC Gdansk|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-10-05|access-date=2017-10-05|language=nl}} The bout was scratched a day before the event due to \"safety concerns,\" as a few [[Lechia Gdańsk]] [[ultras]] – extreme and sometimes violent [[Football hooliganism in Poland|supporters]] of the local football team – showed up just prior to the weigh-ins. Wieczorek is a supporter of [[Ruch Chorzów]], a Lechia Gdańsk rival team. The fighters were absent from ceremonial weigh-ins due to that reason, but the bout was eventually canceled and rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Werdum vs. Tybura]] a month later.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ufc-gdansk-weigh-ins-security-concerns-rowdy-soccer-fans-adam-wieczorek-anthony-hamilton|title=Security concerns over rowdy soccer fans force 2 fighters out of UFC-Gdansk ceremonial weigh-ins|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Moargan|date=2017-10-21|access-date=2017-10-21}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/anthony-hamilton-vs-adam-wieczorek-scratched-from-ufc-gdansk-rescheduled-for-nov-18|title=Anthony Hamilton vs. Adam Wieczorek scratched from UFC-Gdansk, rescheduled for Nov. 18|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-10-22|access-date=2017-10-22}}\n\nPromotional newcomer Przemysław Mysiala was expected to face [[Jimi Manuwa]] at the event.{{cite news|url=http://mmauno.com/jimi-manuwa-vs-przemyslaw-mysiala-in-the-works-for-ufc-gdansk/|title=Jimi Manuwa vs Przemysław Mysiala in the works for UFC Gdansk|publisher=mmauno.com|author=Jim Edwards|date=2017-09-11|access-date=2017-09-11}} However, Mysiala could not resign from [[Absolute Championship Berkut]], therefore the bout never materialized.{{cite news|url=http://www.superlutas.com.br/noticias/70702/escalado-contra-manuwa-polones-e-impedido-de-acertar-com-o-ufc/|title=Scheduled to face Manuwa, Polish is forbidden to join the UFC|publisher=superlutas.com|author=Laerte Viana|date=2017-09-13|access-date=2017-09-28|language=pt}}\n\n[[Teemu Packalén]] was expected to face [[Marcin Held]] at the event. However Packalen pulled out of the fight on October 5 citing a knee injury.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmaviking.com/teemu-packalen-ufc-gdansk-bout/|title=Teemu Packalen out of UFC Gdansk bout|publisher=mmaviking.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-05|access-date=2017-10-05}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer Nasrat Haqparast.{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/nasrat-haqparast-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-tegen-marcin-held-tijdens-ufc-gdansk|title=Nasrat Haqparast takes short notice battle against Marcin Held during UFC Gdansk|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-10-10|access-date=2017-10-10|language=nl}}\n\n[[Trevor Smith (fighter)|Trevor Smith]] was expected to face [[Ramazan Emeev]] at the event. However, Smith pulled out of the bout in early October citing injury and was replaced by [[Sam Alvey]].{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/trevor-smith-geblesseerd-sam-alvey-vecht-tegen-ramazan-emeev-tijdens-ufc-gdansk/|title=Trevor Smith injured, Sam Alvey fights against Ramazan Emeev during UFC Gdansk|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-10-11|access-date=2017-10-11|language=nl}}\n\nOn October 15, it was announced that [[Jim Wallhead]] pulled out of his welterweight bout against [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3]] middleweight winner [[Warlley Alves]] due to an injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/jim-wallhead-out-at-ufc-fight-night-118-promotion-seeks-new-foe-for-warlley-alves|title=Jim Wallhead out at UFC Fight Night 118, promotion seeks new foe for Warlley Alves|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-10-15|access-date=2017-10-16}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Salim Touahri]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/salim-touahri-jim-wallhead-warlley-alves-ufc-fight-night-118-poland|title=Poland's Salim Touahri replaces Jim Wallhead vs. Warlley Alves at UFC Fight Night 118|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-16|access-date=2017-10-16}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Alvey missed the middleweight limit of 185 pounds, coming in at 189 pounds. He was fined 20% of his purse and his bout with Emeev proceeded at a catchweight.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ufc-fight-night-118-official-weigh-in-video-results|title=UFC Fight Night news|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2017/08/ultimate-acerta-lyoto-x-brunson-para-luta-principal-do-ufc-em-sao-paulo.html|title=Ultimate hits Lyoto vs. Brunson for UFC main fight in stemming from an out-of-competition sample collected July 29. Therefore, he was pulled from his UFC debut against Christian Colombo.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/carlos-felipe-flagged-potential-usada-violation-debut-ufc-fight-night-118|title=Carlos Felipe flagged for potential USADA violation, pulled from UFC debut in São Paulo|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-09-19|access-date=2017-09-20}} He was replaced by fellow promotional newcomer [[Marcelo Golm]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/20/16342374/christian-colombo-new-opponent-ufc-sao-paulo|title=Christian Colombo gets new opponent for UFC Sao Paulo|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2017-09-20|access-date=2017-09-20}} On October 20, it was announced that Felipe accepted a two-year suspension retroactive to the date of his provisional suspension. Champion#The Ultimate Fighter 26 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: A New World Champion Finale]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Bisping vs. Gastelum''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 122''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] and held on November 25, 2017, at [[Mercedes-Benz Arena (Shanghai)|Mercedes Benz Arena]] in [[Shanghai]], [[China]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/ufc-makes-mainland-china-debut-with-shanghai-event-on-nov-25|title=UFC makes mainland China debut with Shanghai event on Nov. 25|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=2017-07-30|access-date=2017-07-30}}\n\n==Background==\nAfter previously contesting three events in [[Macau]] (a [[Special administrative regions of China|Special administrative region of China]]) since 2012, the event marked the first that the promotion held on [[Mainland China]].\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]] was expected to serve as the main event.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/8/10/16127884/anderson-silva-vs-kelvin-gastelum-agreed-upon-for-ufc-shanghai|title=Anderson Silva vs. Kelvin Gastelum agreed upon for UFC Shanghai |publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2017-08-10|access-date=2017-08-10}} The pairing was previously scheduled to meet in June 2017 at [[UFC 212]]. However, Gastelum was pulled from the bout after he tested positive for Carboxy-Tetrahydrocannabinol (Carboxy-THC) which is a metabolite of [[marijuana]] or [[hashish]] above the 180 ng/mL allowance by the [[World Anti-Doping Agency]] (WADA) standard.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/kelvin-gastelum-pulled-from-fight-with-anderson-silva-after-testing-positive-for-marijuana-040617|title=Kelvin Gastelum pulled from fight with Anderson Silva after testing positive for marijuana|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2017-04-06|access-date=2017-08-10}} In turn, despite having two months to secure an opponent, Silva and promotion officials confirmed on May 11 that he would not compete at that event.{{cite news|url=http://www.olimpomma.com/anderson-silva-nao-lutara-no-ufc-212/|title=Anderson Silva announces he will not fight at UFC 212|publisher=olimpomma.com|author=Gabriel Carvalho|access-date=2017-05-08|date=2017-08-10|language=pt}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/no-anderson-silva-at-ufc-212|title=Yup, it's official: No Anderson Silva at UFC 212|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-05-11|access-date=2017-08-10}}\n\nOn November 10, it was announced that Silva had failed an out of competition drug test stemming from a sample collected on October 26 and as a result had been pulled from the card.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/11/10/16635354/anderson-silva-fails-usada-drug-test-out-of-ufc-shanghai-main-event|title=Anderson Silva fails USADA drug test, out of UFC Shanghai main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-11-10|access-date=2017-11-10}} A day later, former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter 3]] light heavyweight winner [[Michael Bisping]] was secured as a replacement opponent for Gastelum.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/11/11/16638258/michael-bisping-vs-kelvin-gastelum-targeted-as-new-ufc-shanghai-main-event|title=Michael Bisping vs. Kelvin Gastelum targeted as new UFC Shanghai main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-11-11|access-date=2017-11-11}} Bisping had fought 21 days prior to this event at [[UFC 217]], where he lost the championship to former two-time [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Georges St-Pierre]].{{cite news|url=http://www.latimes.com/sports/la-ufc-217-live-updates-st-pierre-vs-middleweight-title-michael-bisping-vs-1509855433-htmlstory.html|title=UFC 217 live updates: St-Pierre wins middleweight title by submitting Bisping|work=[[Los Angeles Times]]|author=Todd Martin|date=2017-11-04|access-date=2017-11-11}}\n\nOn November 18, it was announced that James Mulheron had failed an out-of-competition drug test stemming from sampled collected on November 10 and as a result he was pulled from the card.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/james-mulheron-pulled-ufc-shanghai-opener-potential-usada-violation|title=James Mulheron pulled from UFC-Shanghai opener due to potential USADA violation|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-11-11|access-date=2017-11-18}} His scheduled opponent, [[Cyril Asker]], remained on the card and faced promotional newcomer [[Hu Yaozong]].{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/yaozong-hu-pakt-5-days-notice-gevecht-tegen-cyril-asker-tijdens-ufc-shanghai/|title=Yaozong Hu takes \"5 days notice\" fight against Cyril Asker during UFC each other at the event. However, Liu pulled out in November and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Song Yadong]].{{cite news|url=https://thefangarage.com/articles/13064-indian-mma-bharat-khandare-reportedly-gets-an-opponent-change-for-ufc-shanghai|title=Indian MMA: Bharat Khandare reportedly gets an opponent change for UFC Shanghai|publisher=thefangarage.com|author=Staff|date=2017-11-19|access-date=2017-11-22}}\n\nAt the weigh ins, [[Rolando Dy]] weighed in at 148 pounds, 2 pounds over the featherweight upper limit of 146 pounds. As such, the bout was held at a catchweight and Dy forfeited 20% of his purse to [[Wuliji Buren]].{{cite one-year suspension due to his failed test. He originally tested positive for [[clomiphene]] and hydroxyclomiphene.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/1/30/16950440/james-mulheron-accepts-one-year-suspension-for-usada-violation|title=James Mulheron accepts one-year suspension for USADA violation|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2018-01-30|access-date=2018-01-30}}\n\nOn February 1, a report came out revealing that Silva tested positive for [[methyltestosterone]] and an unspecified diuretic.{{Cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2018/02/ufc-anderson-silva-postive-drug-test-synthetic-testosterone-diuretic|title=Report: Anderson Silva positive for synthetic testosterone, diuretic in recent failure|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2018-02-01|access-date=2018-02-01}} On July 18, Silva was suspended for one-year, retroactive to November 10, 2017, after USADA traced his second positive drug test to a compounding pharmacy known for producing tainted supplements.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/usada-suspends-anderson-silva-one-year-after-tainted-supplement-found|title=USADA suspends Anderson Silva one year after tainted supplement found|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2018-07-18|access-date=2018-07-18}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2017 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n{{UFC arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on November 11, 2017, at [[Ted Constant Convocation Center]] in [[Norfolk, Virginia]].{{cite news|url=https://pilotonline.com/sports/other/ufc-s-headed-to-shanghai-sydney-sao-paulo-and-norfolk/article_92e58283-d6a2-5fc6-be35-c0674b455be8.html|title=UFC's headed to Shanghai, Sydney, Sao Paulo event marked the first time that the promotion visited the [[Hampton Roads]] area of Virginia.\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Dustin Poirier]] and former [[WEC Lightweight Championship|WEC]] and [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Anthony Pettis]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/trainer-ex-champ-anthony-pettis-vs-dustin-poirier-at-ufc-fight-night-120-in-virginia|title=Ex-champ Anthony Pettis vs. Dustin Poirier at UFC Fight Night 120 in linked to serve as the co-headliner at this event.{{cite news|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2017/08/recuperado-de-lesao-minotouro-vai-enfrentar-cannonier-no-ufc-norfolk.html|title=Recovered from injury, Minotouro will face Cannonier at The bout proceeded at a catchweight and Lopez forfeited 20% of his purse to [[Raphael Assunção]].{{Cite Night: Werdum vs. Tybura","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|2017 mixed martial arts event}}\n{{Use American English|date=November 2017}}\n{{Use dmy dates|date=November 2017}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC Fight Night: Werdum vs. Tybura\n|image=UFC_FN_121_Poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2017|11|19}} \n|venue= [[Qudos Bank Arena]]\n|city= [[Sydney]], Australia\n|attendance= 10,021{{cite Sydney|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=19 November 2017|date=19 November 2017}}\n|previousevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Poirier vs. Pettis]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Bisping vs. Gastelum]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Werdum vs. Tybura''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 121''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on November 19, 2017, at [[Qudos Bank Arena]] in [[Sydney]], Australia.{{cite news|url=http://www.dailytelegraph.com.au/sport/ufc-in-australia-sydney-to-host-event-at-qudos-bank-arena-in-november-after-three-year-wait/news-story/e195793dd74cdf9a22c38969da570238|title=UFC in Australia: Sydney to host event at Qudos Bank Arena in November after three year wait|work=The Daily Telegraph|location=Australia|author=Nick Walshaw|access-date=8 July 2017|date=8 July 2017}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event marked the promotion's fifth visit to Sydney, and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Rockhold vs. Bisping]] in November 2014.\n\nA heavyweight bout between the [[K-1 World Grand Prix 2001 Final|2001 K-1 World Grand Prix]] winner and former interim title contender [[Mark Hunt]] against [[Marcin Tybura]] was expected to serve as the event's headliner.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/08/mark-hunt-marcin-tybura-ufc-fight-night-sydney|title=Mark Hunt to headline UFC Fight Night in Sydney vs. Marcin Tybura|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=12 August 2017|access-date=12 August 2017}} However, on October 10, Hunt was removed from the bout due to \"medical concerns\". He had recently admitted in an article trouble sleeping and said he's begin to stutter and slur his words. The UFC released a statement claiming that \"the health-related statements made by Hunt represented the first time the UFC was made aware of these claims\". They also announced that it would be required that Hunt undergo further testing and evaluations prior to competing in any future bout. As a result, former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Fabrício Werdum]] was chosen as his replacement.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ufc-medical-concerns-pulls-mark-hunt-ufc-fight-night-121-fabricio-werdum|title=UFC cites 'medical concerns,' pulls Mark Hunt from UFC Fight Night 121 in favor of Fabricio Werdum|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=10 October 2017|access-date=11 October 2017}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption]] welterweight winner [[Jesse Taylor]] was expected to face [[Belal Muhammad]] at the event. However, on September 13, it was announced that Taylor was pulled from the card after being notified by [[USADA]] of a potential doping violation. He was flagged after an out-of-competition drug test conducted August 22.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Statement-on-Jesse-Taylor|title=UFC statement on Jesse Taylor|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=13 September 2017|access-date=13 September 2017}} He was replaced by [[Tim Means]].{{cite news|url=https://mma-today.com/exclusive-tim-means-vs-belal-muhammad-works-ufc-sydney|title=Tim Means vs. Belal Muhammad in the works for UFC Sydney|publisher=mma-today.com|author=Shawn Bitter|date=11 October 2017|access-date=11 October 2017|archive-date=11 October 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171011233114/https://mma-today.com/exclusive-tim-means-vs-belal-muhammad-works-ufc-Sydney|url-status=dead}} On October 13, USADA officially suspended Taylor one year for testing positive for anti-estrogen agent [[clomiphene]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/10/13/16472374/usada-suspends-jesse-taylor-one-year-for-positive-drug-test-for-clomiphene|title=USADA suspends Jesse Taylor one year for positive drug test for clomiphene|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=13 October 2017|access-date=13 October 2017}}\n\n[[Jeremy Kennedy]] was expected to face [[Alexander Volkanovski]] at the event. However, Kennedy pulled out of the fight on October 5, citing a back injury.{{cite news|url=https://mma-today.com/exclusive-jeremy-kennedy-suffers-back-injury-ufc-sydney|title=Jeremy Kennedy suffers back injury; out of UFC Sydney|publisher=mma-today.com|author=Nolan King|date=5 October 2017|access-date=5 October 2017|archive-date=6 October 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171006212409/https://mma-today.com/exclusive-jeremy-kennedy-suffers-back-injury-ufc-sydney|url-status=dead}} He was replaced by [[Humberto Bandenay]].{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/humberto-bandenay-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-tegen-alexander-volkanovski-in-sydney/|title=Humberto Bandenay takes short notice battle against Alexander Volkanovski in Sydney|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=11 October 2017|access-date=11 October 2017|language=nl}} In turn, Bandenay pulled out of the fight on 6 November and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Shane Young]]. The bout took place at a catchweight of 150 lb.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/humberto-bandenay-out-ufc-sydney-against-alex-volkanovski|title=Humberto Bandenay out at UFC-Sydney; featherweight sought for Alex Volkanovski|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=7 November 2017|access-date=7 November 2017}}{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/shane-young-maakt-short-notice-debuut-tegen-alexander-volkanovski-tijdens-ufc-sydney|title=Shane Young makes short note debut against Alexander Volkanovski during UFC Sydney|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=12 November 2017|access-date=13 November 2017|language=nl}}\n\nA lightweight bout between former [[List of Bellator champions#Lightweight Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]] [[Will Brooks]] and [[Nik Lentz]] was previously scheduled for [[UFC 216]]. However, the pairing was scrapped during the weigh-ins for the event, as Lentz was stricken with \"medical issues\" and deemed unfit to compete.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ufc-216-loses-featured-prelim-will-brook-nik-lentz|title=UFC 216 loses a featured prelim on eve of event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=6 October 2017|access-date=16 October 2017}} The fight took place at this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/will-brooks-nik-lentz-targeted-ufc-fight-night-121-sydney-australia|title=Will Brooks vs. Nik Lentz targeted for UFC Fight Night 121|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=16 October 2017|access-date=16 October 2017}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between promotional newcomer [[Adam Wieczorek]] and [[Anthony Hamilton (fighter)|Anthony Hamilton]] was initially scheduled to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Cowboy vs. Till]]. However, the bout was removed a day before the event due to \"safety concerns,\" as a few [[Lechia Gdańsk]] [[ultras]] – extreme and sometimes violent [[Football hooliganism in Poland|supporters]] of the local football team – showed up just prior to the weigh-ins. Wieczorek is a supporter of [[Ruch Chorzów]], a Lechia Gdańsk rival team. The fighters were absent from ceremonial weigh-ins due to that reason, but the bout was eventually cancelled and rescheduled for this event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ufc-gdansk-weigh-ins-security-concerns-rowdy-soccer-fans-adam-wieczorek-anthony-hamilton|title=Security concerns over rowdy soccer fans force 2 fighters out of UFC-Gdansk ceremonial weigh-ins|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=21 October 2017|access-date=21 October 2017}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/anthony-hamilton-vs-adam-wieczorek-scratched-from-ufc-gdansk-rescheduled-for-nov-18|title=Anthony Hamilton vs. Adam Wieczorek scratched from UFC-Gdansk, rescheduled for Nov. 18|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=22 October 2017|access-date=22 October 2017}}\n\n[[Bec Rawlings]] was originally scheduled to compete against [[Joanne Calderwood]] at the event. However, on November 7, it was announced that Calderwood withdrew from the event due to \"undisclosed reasons\". She was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Jessica-Rose Clark]].{{cite news|url=https://mma-today.com/exclusive-joanne-calderwood-jessy-rose-clark-steps-take-bec-rawlingsm|title=Exclusive: Joanne Calderwood out – Jessy Rose-Clark steps in to take on Bec Rawlings|publisher=mma-today.com|author=Shawn Bitter|date=7 November 2017|access-date=8 November 2017}}{{Dead link|date=July 2025 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=yes }}\n\n[[Jessica-Rose Clark]], [[Ryan Benoit]], [[Frank Camacho]], and [[Nadia Kassem]] missed weight at the official weigh-ins, coming in at 128, 129, 160 and 120 pounds, respectively. Rose-Clark and Benoit forfeited 20% and Camacho and Kassem forfeited 30% of their purses to their opponents and the bouts proceeded as catchweight bouts.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/ufc-fight-night-121-sydney-early-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC Fight Night 121 official weigh-in results: Headliner set, but four fighters heavy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=17 November 2017|access-date=17 November 2017}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent Silva vs. Bisping]] and later [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Edwards]], [[UFC Fight Night: Vieira vs. Tate]]and [[UFC on ABC: Hill vs. Rountree Jr.]] for the most decisions at a single UFC event with 10. The record was later surpassed by [[UFC 263]] which had eleven decision bouts.\n\nThis event also became the longest [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] event with 3:04:18 of fight time, passing [[UFC Fight Night: Machida vs. Mousasi]] with 2:53:32. The record was since surpassed by [[UFC 251]] with 3:07:27.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/ufc-fight-night-121-post-event-facts-a-long-night-leads-to-a-dubious-new-record|title=UFC Fight Fox: Lawler vs. dos Anjos\n|image=UFC_Winnipeg_poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2017|12|16}}\n|venue= [[Bell MTS Place]]\n|city= [[Winnipeg, Manitoba|Winnipeg]], [[Manitoba]], [[Canada]]\n|attendance=8,862{{Cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/12/ufc-on-fox-26-draws-reported-8862-attendance-for-781k-live-gate|title=UFC on FOX 26 draws reported 8,862 attendance for $781k live gate|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-12-16|access-date=2017-12-17}}\n|gate=$781,359\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Swanson vs. Ortega]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 219|UFC 219: Cyborg vs. Holm]]\n}}\n'''''UFC on Fox: Lawler vs. dos Anjos''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 26''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on December 16, 2017, at the [[Bell MTS Place]] in [[Winnipeg, Manitoba|Winnipeg]], [[Manitoba]], [[Canada]].{{cite news|url=https://mma-today.com/december-ufc-fight-night-event-works-winnipeg|title=December \"UFC Fight Night\" Event in the works for Winnipeg|publisher=mma-today.com|author=Nolan King|date=2017-07-26|access-date=2017-07-26|archive-date=2017-07-28|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170728074424/https://mma-today.com/december-ufc-fight-night-event-works-winnipeg|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the promotion has hosted in Winnipeg, following [[UFC 161]] in June 2013.\n\nThe event was headlined by a welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Robbie Lawler]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Rafael dos Anjos]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Former-Champs-Collide-as-UFC-Returns-to-Winnipeg|title=Former champs collide as UFC returns to Winnipeg|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-12|access-date=2017-10-12}} On October 13, UFC President [[Dana White]] announced that the winner of the main event would receive a title shot against current champion [[Tyron Woodley]], who won the belt by defeating Lawler by first-round knockout at [[UFC 201]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmaweekly.com/robbie-lawler-vs-rafael-dos-anjos-winner-gets-next-title-shot-against-tyron-woodley|title=Robbie Lawler vs. Rafael dos Anjos winner gets next title shot against Tyron Woodley|publisher=mmaweekly.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2017-10-13|access-date=2017-10-13}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]] and [[Jared Cannonier]], briefly linked to [[UFC Fight Night: Poirier vs. Pettis]], was instead scheduled for this event.{{cite news|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2017/08/recuperado-de-lesao-minotouro-vai-enfrentar-cannonier-no-ufc-norfolk.html|title=Recovered from injury, Minotouro will face Cannonier at UFC Norfolk|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=André Azevedo, Marcelo Barone and Raphael Marinho|date=2017-08-10|access-date=2017-08-10|language=pt}}{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Nogueira-Cannonier-heads-to-Winnipeg|title=Nogueira-Cannonier heads to Winnipeg|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2017-08-17|access-date=2017-08-17}} However, on October 19, it was announced that Nogueira was pulled from the card after being notified by [[USADA]] of a potential doping violation, which resulted from an out-of-competition sample collected on September 27. The violation was due to a positive test for [[hydrochlorothiazide]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/antonio-rogerio-nogueira-potential-usada-violation-claims-innocence-ufc-on-fox-26|title=Antonio Rogerio Nogueira hit with potential USADA violation, pulled from UFC on FOX 26|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2017-10-19|access-date=2017-10-19}} He was replaced by [[Jan Błachowicz]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/danny-roberts-sheldon-westcott-jan-blachowicz-jared-cannonier-ufc-on-fox-26-winnipeg|title=Roberts vs. Westcott, Blachowicz vs. Cannonier added to UFC on FOX 26 in Winnipeg|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-11-07|access-date=2017-11-07}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Glover Teixeira]] and [[Misha Cirkunov]] was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Machida]], but was moved to this event after a recent hand injury for Teixeira was slow to heal and required surgery.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/9/12/16298498/glover-teixeira-misha-cirkunov-moved-sao-paulo-ufc-on-fox-26|title=Glover Teixeira vs. Misha Cirkunov moved to UFC on FOX 26|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2017-09-12|access-date=2017-09-13}}\n\nFormer [[List of WEC champions#Featherweight Championship|WEC Featherweight Champion]] and two-time [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[José Aldo]] was scheduled to face former title challenger [[Ricardo Lamas]] in a rematch in the co-headliner.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/jose-aldo-vs-ricardo-lamas-rematch-co-headlines-ufc-on-fox-26-perry-ponzinibbio-mein-silva-added|title=Jose Aldo vs. Ricardo Lamas rematch co-headlines UFC on FOX 26; Perry-Ponzinibbio, Mein-Silva added|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-13|access-date=2017-11-11}} The pairing met previously at [[UFC 169]] in February 2014, with Aldo defending his title via unanimous decision.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/ufc-169-results-photos-jose-aldo-retains-title-outkicks-ricardo-lamas|title=UFC 169 results, photos: Jose Aldo retains title, outkicks Ricardo Lamas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Ben Fowlkes|date=2014-02-02|access-date=2017-11-13}} However, Aldo was removed from the bout on November 10 in favor of a rematch against the current champion [[Max Holloway]] two weeks earlier at [[UFC 218]] after Holloway's scheduled opponent, former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Frankie Edgar]] pulled out of that fight.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/11/11/16633192/max-holloway-vs-jose-aldo-2-slated-as-new-ufc-218-main-event|title=Max Holloway vs. Jose Aldo 2 slated as new UFC 218 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-11-11|access-date=2017-11-11}} He was replaced by [[Josh Emmett]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmanews.com/ufc-on-fox-26-ricardo-lamas-meets-josh-emmett-in-winnipeg/|title=UFC on FOX 26: Ricardo Lamas meets Josh Emmett in Winnipeg|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Dana Becker|date=2017-11-27|access-date=2017-11-28}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Vitor Miranda]] and promotional newcomer [[Julian Marquez]] was expected to take place at this event. However, on December 4, it was announced that Miranda was pulled from the fight, citing injury and was replaced by [[Darren Stewart]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/12/5/16738000/several-changes-announced-for-ufc-winnipeg|title=Several changes announced for UFC on FOX 26|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Peter Carroll|date=2017-12-04|access-date=2017-12-04}}\n\n[[Sultan Aliev]] and [[Sheldon Westcott]] were removed out of their respective fights against [[Nordine Taleb]] and [[Danny Roberts (fighter)|Danny Roberts]] for undisclosed reasons. Taleb and Roberts were then scheduled to face each other.\n\nA flyweight bout between former title challenger and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Tournament of Champions]] flyweight winner [[Tim Elliott]] and [[Justin Scoggins]] was expected to take place at this event. However, on December 3, it was announced that Scoggins was pulled from the fight due to a spinal fracture injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer Pietro Menga.{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/justin-scoggins-out-tim-elliott-nu-tegen-pietro-menga/|title=Justin Scoggins out: Tim Elliott now faces Pietro Menga|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Stefan Van Erp|date=2017-12-03|access-date=2017-12-04|language=nl}} In turn, Menga also withdrew from the bout during fight week following weight-cutting complications and the bout was scrapped entirely.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/12/flyweight-pietro-menga-doesnt-weigh-in-for-ufc-on-fox-26-fight-with-tim-elliott-scrapped|title=Flyweight Pietro Menga doesn't weigh in for UFC on FOX 26, fight with Tim Elliott scrapped|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson and Mike Bohn|date=2017-12-15|access-date=2017-12-15}}\n\nAt the weigh ins, Emmett weighed in at 148.5 pounds, 2.5 pounds over the featherweight non-title fight upper limit of 146 pounds. As such, the bout proceeded at a catchweight and Emmett is expected to forfeit part of his purse to his opponent Lamas. Meanwhile, Menga did not weigh in and his fight against Elliott was canceled.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/12/ufc-on-fox-26-winnipeg-robbie-lawler-rafael-dos-anjos-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC on FOX 26 weigh-in results: Headliners official, one fighter over, one fight scratched|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-12-15|access-date=2017-12-15}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox)}}\n{{MMAevent 218: Holloway vs. Aldo 2]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on Fox: Lawler vs. dos Anjos]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Swanson vs. Ortega''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 123''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on December 9, 2017, at [[Save Mart Center]] in [[Fresno, California]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Fight-Night-123-Marks-Promotions-Fresno-Debut-on-Dec-9-127023|title=UFC Fight Night 123 marks promotion's first visit to Fresno|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2017-09-19|access-date=2017-09-19}}\n\n==Background==\nWhile the UFC has hosted many events in [[Northern California]], the event marked the promotion's first visit to Fresno. Former [[Zuffa]] subsidiary [[Strikeforce (mixed martial arts)|Strikeforce]] previously contested five events in Fresno, the most recent in [[2010 in Strikeforce#Strikeforce Challengers: Bowling vs. Voelker|October 2010]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/09/ufc-to-debut-in-fresno-on-december-9-with-ufc-fight-night-123|title=UFC to debut in Fresno on Dec. 9 with UFC Fight Night 123 |publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-09-17|access-date=2017-09-19}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Cub Swanson]] and [[Brian Ortega]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmanews.com/report-cub-swanson-vs-brian-ortega-in-the-works-for-ufc-fight-night-123/|title=Cub Swanson vs. Brian Ortega in the works for UFC Fight Night 123|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Fernando Quiles Jr.|date=2017-09-28|access-date=2017-09-28}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/cub-swanson-brian-ortega-ufc-fight-night-113-fresno-main-event|title=Cub Swanson says he's headlining UFC Fight Night 123 against Brian Ortega|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-10-02|access-date=2017-10-02}}{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Swanson-faces-Ortega-in-All-Cali-main-event-in-Fresno|title=Swanson faces Ortega in All-California main event in Fresno|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2017-10-10|access-date=2017-10-10}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[John Phillips (fighter)|John Phillips]] was expected to face [[Eryk Anders]] at the event. However, Phillips was removed from the card on November 2 and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Markus Perez Echeimberg]].{{cite news|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/markus-maluko-assina-com-o-ufc-e-estreia-contra-eryk-anders-9-de-dezembro.ghtml|title=Markus Maluko signs with UFC and debuts against Eryk Anders, December 9|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|access-date=2017-11-02|date=2017-11-02|language=pt}}\n\n[[Rani Yahya]] was expected to face [[Aljamain Sterling]] at this event. However, Yahya was forced to pull out from the card on November 7 due to injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/aljamain-sterling-new-opponent-ufc-fresno-rani-yahya-out|title=Aljamain Sterling needs new opponent for UFC-Fresno with Rani Yahya forced out|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-11-07|access-date=2017-11-08}} He was replaced by former [[WSOF Bantamweight Championship|WSOF Bantamweight Champion]] [[Marlon Moraes]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/aljamain-sterling-vs-marlon-moraes-ufc-fresno|title=Aljamain Sterling gets Marlon Moraes as UFC-Fresno replacement opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2017-11-17|access-date=2017-11-17}}\n\n[[Bryan Caraway]] was expected to face [[Luke Sanders]] at the event. However, on November 20, Caraway pulled out of the fight due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/ufc-fresno-bryan-caraway-out-luke-sanders-needs-new-opponent|title=Bryan Caraway out at UFC-Fresno, Luke Sanders needs new opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-11-20|access-date=2017-11-20}} He was replaced by [[Andre Soukhamthath]].{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/andre-soukhamthath-vervangt-bryan-caraway-tegen-luke-sanders-tijdens-ufc-fresno/|title=Andre Soukhamthath replaces Bryan Caraway against Luke Sanders at UFC Fresno|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-11-22|access-date=2017-11-22|language=nl}}\n\nCarls John de Tomas and [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] were expected to meet in a flyweight bout at this event. However, the [[California State Athletic Commission|CSAC]] elected to move the bout to the bantamweight division as they were concerned with De Tomas weight during fight week.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/12/ufc-csac-shift-weight-classes-ufc-fresno-bout|title=UFC, CSAC shift weight class on UFC-Fresno bout|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-12-07|access-date=2017-12-07}}\n\n[[Benito Lopez]] was fined 10% of his show money by the CSAC due to shoving his opponent [[Albert Morales]] at the weigh-ins ceremony. That money will go directly to the commission.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/12/benito-lopez-fined-ufc-fresno-weigh-ins-shove-albert-morales-csac|title=Benito Lopez fined 10 percent for UFC-Fresno weigh-ins shove; CSAC to keep money|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-12-08|access-date=2017-12-08}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports awards==\nThe following fighters were awarded $50,000 bonuses:{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/12/ufc-fight-night-123-bonuses-brian-ortegas-amazing-performance-doubles-up-for-100000|title=UFC Ortega vs. Cub Swanson'''\n*'''Performance of the Night:''' '''Brian Ortega''' and '''Marlon Moraes'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[California State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $866,000.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/12/ufc-fight-night-123-fresno-cub-salaries-brian-ortega-cub-swanson-fight-purses|title=UFC Fight Night 123 salaries: Ex-WSOF champ Marlon Moraes leads $866k payroll|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-12-13|access-date=2017-12-13}}\n* '''Brian Ortega:''' $58,000 (includes $29,000 win bonus) def. Cub Swanson: $76,000\n* '''Gabriel Benítez:''' $28,000 (includes $14,000 win bonus) def. Jason Knight: $31,000\n* '''Marlon Moraes:''' $146,000 (includes $73,000 win bonus) def. Aljamian Sterling: $36,000\n* '''Scott Holtzman:''' $40,000 (includes $20,000 win bonus) def. Darrell Horcher: $15,000\n* '''Eryk Anders:''' $28,000 (includes $14,000 win bonus) def. Markus Perez: $12,000\n* '''Benito Lopez:''' $19,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Albert Morales: $15,000 ^\n* '''Alexis Davis:''' $64,000 (includes $32,000 win bonus) def. Liz Carmouche: $30,000\n* '''Andre Soukhamthath:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Luke Sanders: $12,000 \n* '''Alex Perez:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Carls John de Tomas: $10,000\n* '''Frankie Saenz:''' $40,000 (includes $20,000 win bonus) def. Merab Dvalishvili: $10,000\n* '''Alejandro Pérez:''' $46,000 (includes $23,000 win bonus) def. Antônio Braga Neto: $10,000\n\n^ ''Benito Lopez was fined 10 percent of his purse ($1,000) for shoving his opponent at the weigh-ins ceremony. That money went directly to the commission.''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn December 29, it was announced that Carl John de Tomas failed a [[USADA]] drug test stemming from his appearance at this event following his weigh-in.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/12/ufc-carls-john-de-tomas-fails-usada-drug-test|title=Carls John de Tomas fails USADA drug test, notified of potential anti-doping infraction|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2017-12-29|access-date=2017-12-29}} On February 5, he accepted a one-year suspension.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2018/02/former-ufc-bantamweight-carls-john-de-tomas-accepts-one-year-usada-suspension|title=Former UFC bantamweight Carls John de Tomas accepts one-year USADA suspension|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2018-02-05|access-date=2018-02-05}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2017 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n{{UFC Events}}\n\n[[Category:UFC Fight Night]]\n[[Category:2017 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts in California]]\n[[Category:Sports competitions in Fresno, California]]\n[[Category:December 2017 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:2017 in sports in California]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC Fight Night: Swanson vs. Ortega"}]}]}}
